```
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    92→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,
    95→        target=normalized_target,
    96→    )
    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
   101→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   102→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   103→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   104→
   105→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   106→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   107→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   108→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   110→            payload["integrity_penalty"] = "target_match_reset"
   111→        else:
   112→            adjusted[dimension] = {
   113→                "score": 0.0,
   114→                "integrity_penalty": "target_match_reset",
   115→            }
   116→
   117→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   118→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   119→    return adjusted, meta

   122→def _aggregate_scores(
   123→    dim_scores: dict, compute_health_score_fn
   124→) -> dict[str, float]:
   125→    """Derive the 4 aggregate scores from dimension-level data."""
   126→    mechanical = {
   127→        n: d
   128→        for n, d in dim_scores.items()
   129→        if "subjective_assessment" not in d.get("detectors", {})
   130→    }
   131→    return {
   132→        "overall_score": compute_health_score_fn(dim_scores),
   133→        "strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   134→            dim_scores, score_key="strict_score"
   135→        ),
   136→        "objective_score": compute_health_score_fn(mechanical),
   137→        "verified_strict_score": compute_health_score_fn(
   138→            mechanical, [REDACTED]
   139→        ),
   140→    }

   143→def _active_scan_coverage(state: StateModel) -> ScanCoverageRecord:
   144→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
   145→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict) or not scan_coverage:
   146→        return {}
   147→
   148→    lang_name = state.get("lang")
   149→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
   150→        payload = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
   151→        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
   152→
   153→    if len(scan_coverage) == 1:
   154→        only = next(iter(scan_coverage.values()))
   155→        return only if isinstance(only, dict) else {}
   156→    return {}
   157→
   158→
   159→def _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   160→    state: StateModel,
   161→    *,
   162→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict],
   163→) -> None:
   164→    coverage_payload = _active_scan_coverage(state)
   165→    detectors_payload = coverage_payload.get("detectors", {})
   166→    if not isinstance(detectors_payload, dict):
   167→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   168→            "status": "full",
   169→            "confidence": 1.0,
   170→            "detectors": [],
   171→            "dimensions": [],
   172→        }
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    reduced_detectors: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   176→    for detector, raw in detectors_payload.items():
   177→        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
   178→            continue
   179→        status = str(raw.get("status", "full")).lower()
   180→        confidence = coerce_confidence(raw.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   181→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
   182→            continue
   183→        reduced_detectors[str(detector)] = dict(raw)
   184→
   185→    reduced_dimensions: list[str] = []
   186→    score_confidence_detectors: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   187→    for detector, payload in reduced_detectors.items():
   188→        score_confidence_detectors.append(
   189→            {
   190→                "detector": detector,
   191→                "status": str(payload.get("status", "reduced")),
   192→                "confidence": round(
   193→                    coerce_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0), 2
   194→                ),
   195→                "summary": str(payload.get("summary", "") or ""),
   196→                "impact": str(payload.get("impact", "") or ""),
   197→                "remediation": str(payload.get("remediation", "") or ""),
   198→                "tool": str(payload.get("tool", "") or ""),
   199→                "reason": str(payload.get("reason", "") or ""),
   200→            }
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    for dim_name, dim_data in dimension_scores.items():
   204→        if not isinstance(dim_data, dict):
   205→            continue
   206→        detectors = dim_data.get("detectors", {})
   207→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   208→            continue
   209→
   210→        impacts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   211→        for detector_name, detector_meta in detectors.items():
   212→            reduced = reduced_detectors.get(str(detector_name))
   213→            if not isinstance(detector_meta, dict):
   214→                continue
   215→            if reduced is None:
   216→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_status", None)
   217→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   218→                detector_meta.pop("coverage_summary", None)
   219→                continue
   220→            confidence = coerce_confidence(reduced.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   221→            status = str(reduced.get("status", "reduced"))
   222→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   223→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status
   224→            detector_meta["coverage_confidence"] = round(confidence, 2)
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   226→            impacts.append(
   227→                {
   228→                    "detector": str(detector_name),
   229→                    "status": status,
   230→                    "confidence": round(confidence, 2),
   231→                    "summary": summary,
   232→                }
   233→            )
   234→
   235→        if not impacts:
   236→            dim_data.pop("coverage_status", None)
   237→            dim_data.pop("coverage_confidence", None)
   238→            dim_data.pop("coverage_impacts", None)
   239→            continue
   240→
   241→        reduced_dimensions.append(str(dim_name))
   242→        dim_data["coverage_status"] = "reduced"
   243→        dim_data["coverage_confidence"] = round(
   244→            min(coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0) for item in impacts),
   245→            2,
   246→        )
   247→        dim_data["coverage_impacts"] = impacts
   248→
   249→    if not score_confidence_detectors:
   250→        state["score_confidence"] = {
   251→            "status": "full",
   252→            "confidence": 1.0,
   253→            "detectors": [],
   254→            "dimensions": [],
   255→        }
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    state["score_confidence"] = {
   259→        "status": "reduced",
   260→        "confidence": round(
   261→            min(
   262→                coerce_confidence(item.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
   263→                for item in score_confidence_detectors
   264→            ),
   265→            2,
   266→        ),
   267→        "detectors": score_confidence_detectors,
   268→        "dimensions": sorted(set(reduced_dimensions)),
   269→    }
   270→
   271→
   272→def _update_objective_health(
   273→    state: StateModel,
   274→    findings: dict,
   275→    *,
   276→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   277→) -> None:
   278→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   279→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   280→    if not pots:
   281→        return
   282→
   283→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   284→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   285→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   286→        compute_health_score,
   287→        compute_score_bundle,
   288→        merge_potentials,
   289→    )
   290→
   291→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   292→    if not merged:
   293→        return
   294→
   295→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   296→    integrity_target = (
   297→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   298→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   299→        else None
   300→    )
   301→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   302→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   303→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   304→            subjective_assessments,
   305→            target=integrity_target,
   306→        )
   307→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   308→
   309→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   310→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   311→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   312→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   313→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   314→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   316→        return
   317→
   318→    # Derive language-scoped subjective dimensions so only dimensions with
   319→    # a review pathway get 0-score placeholders.  Explicit assessments
   320→    # outside this set still count (append_subjective_dimensions handles
   321→    # that).  Without this, all 20 global default dimensions would get
   322→    # placeholders — including ones the language's review never covers.
   323→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   324→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   325→    if lang_name:
   326→        try:
   327→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.lang import (
   328→                HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG,
   329→            )
   330→
   331→            lang_dims = HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG.get(lang_name)
   332→            if lang_dims:
   333→                allowed_subjective = set(lang_dims)
   334→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   335→            pass
   336→
   337→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   338→        findings,
   339→        merged,
   340→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   341→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   342→    )
   343→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   344→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   345→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   346→
   347→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   348→
   349→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   350→        name: dict(
   351→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   352→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   355→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   356→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   357→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   358→        )
   359→        for name in lenient_scores
   360→    }
   361→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   362→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   363→
   364→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   365→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   366→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   367→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   368→            continue
   369→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   370→            continue
   371→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   372→            continue
   373→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   374→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   375→        carried.setdefault(
   376→            "verified_strict_score",
   377→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   378→        )
   379→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   380→
   381→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   382→        state,
   383→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   387→
   388→
   389→def _recompute_stats(
   390→    state: StateModel,
   391→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   392→    *,
   393→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   394→) -> None:
   395→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   396→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   397→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   398→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   399→    state["stats"] = {
   400→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   401→        **counters,
   402→        "by_tier": {
   403→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   404→        },
   405→    }
   406→    _update_objective_health(
   407→        state,
   408→        findings,
   409→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   410→    )
   411→
   412→
   413→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   414→    return {
   415→        "last_ignored": 0,
   416→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   417→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   418→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   419→        "recent_scans": 0,
   420→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   421→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   422→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   423→    }
   424→
   425→
   426→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   427→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   428→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   429→    if not history:
   430→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   431→
   432→    scans_with_suppression = [
   433→        entry
   434→        for entry in history
   435→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   436→        and (
   437→            "ignored" in entry
   438→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   439→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   440→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   441→        )
   442→    ]
   443→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   444→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   445→
   446→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   447→    last = recent[-1]
   448→
   449→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   450→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   451→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   452→
   453→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   454→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   455→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   456→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   457→    else:
   458→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   459→
   460→    return {
   461→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   462→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   463→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   464→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   465→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   466→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   467→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   468→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   469→    }
   470→
```

```
1→"""State persistence and migration routines."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import cast
    11→
    12→__all__ = [
    13→    "load_state",
    14→    "save_state",
    15→]
    16→
    17→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    CURRENT_VERSION,
    20→    STATE_FILE,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    empty_state,
    23→    ensure_state_defaults,
    24→    json_default,
    25→    validate_state_invariants,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    28→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    34→    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    35→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    36→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    37→    return data
    38→
    39→
    40→def _normalize_loaded_state(data: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    41→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    42→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    43→    ensure_state_defaults(data)
    44→    normalized = cast(StateModel, data)
    45→    validate_state_invariants(normalized)
    46→    return normalized
    47→
    48→
    49→def load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel:
    50→    """Load state from disk, or return empty state on missing/corruption."""
    51→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    52→    if not state_path.exists():
    53→        return empty_state()
    54→
    55→    try:
    56→        data = _load_json(state_path)
    57→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as ex:
    58→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    59→        if backup.exists():
    60→            try:
    61→                backup_data = _load_json(backup)
    62→                print(
    63→                    f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.",
    64→                    file=sys.stderr,
    65→                )
    66→                return _normalize_loaded_state(backup_data)
    67→            except (
    68→                json.JSONDecodeError,
    69→                UnicodeDecodeError,
    70→                OSError,
    71→                ValueError,
    72→                TypeError,
    73→                AttributeError,
    74→            ) as backup_ex:
    75→                logger.debug("Backup state load failed from %s: %s", backup, backup_ex)
    76→
    77→        print(f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    78→        rename_failed = False
    79→        try:
    80→            state_path.rename(state_path.with_suffix(".json.corrupted"))
    81→        except OSError as rename_ex:
    82→            rename_failed = True
    83→            logger.debug(
    84→                "Failed to rename corrupted state file %s: %s", state_path, rename_ex
    85→            )
    86→        if rename_failed:
    87→            logger.debug(
    88→                "Corrupted state file retained at original path: %s", state_path
    89→            )
    90→        return empty_state()
    91→
    92→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    93→    if version > CURRENT_VERSION:
    94→        print(
    95→            "  ⚠ State file version "
    96→            f"{version} is newer than supported ({CURRENT_VERSION}). "
    97→            "Some features may not work correctly.",
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    try:
   102→        return _normalize_loaded_state(data)
   103→    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as normalize_ex:
   104→        print(
   105→            f"  ⚠ State invariants invalid ({normalize_ex}). Starting fresh.",
   106→            file=sys.stderr,
   107→        )
   108→        return empty_state()
   109→
   110→
   111→def _coerce_integrity_target(value: object) -> float | None:
   112→    if not is_numeric(value):
   113→        return None
   114→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
   115→
   116→
   117→def _resolve_integrity_target(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    explicit_target: float | None,
   120→) -> float | None:
   121→    target = _coerce_integrity_target(explicit_target)
   122→    if target is not None:
   123→        return target
   124→
   125→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   126→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   127→        return None
   128→    return _coerce_integrity_target(integrity.get("target_score"))
   129→
   130→
   131→def save_state(
   132→    state: StateModel,
   133→    path: Path | None = None,
   134→    *,
   135→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   136→) -> None:
   137→    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
   138→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   139→    _recompute_stats(
   140→        state,
   141→        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   142→        subjective_integrity_target=_resolve_integrity_target(
   143→            state,
   144→            subjective_integrity_target,
   145→        ),
   146→    )
   147→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   148→
   149→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
   150→    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   151→
   152→    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   153→
   154→    if state_path.exists():
   155→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   156→        try:
   157→            shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))
   158→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   159→            logger.debug(
   160→                "Failed to create state backup %s: %s",
   161→                state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak"),
   162→                backup_ex,
   163→            )
   164→
   165→    try:
   166→        safe_write_text(state_path, content)
   167→    except OSError as ex:
   168→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save state: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   169→        raise
   170→
```

```
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (
    19→    _append_scan_history,
    20→    _build_merge_diff,
    21→    _compute_suppression,
    22→    _merge_scan_inputs,
    23→    _record_scan_metadata,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    26→    ScanDiff,
    27→    StateModel,
    28→    ensure_state_defaults,
    29→    utc_now,

    32→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
```

```
1→"""Core query payload writing helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query, load_config
    11→from desloppify.core.file_paths import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    13→from desloppify.state import json_default
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def write_query(data: dict, *, query_file: Path) -> OutputResult:
    19→    """Write structured query payloads with config context and graceful fallback."""
    20→    if "config" not in data:
    21→        try:
    22→            data["config"] = config_for_query(load_config())
    23→        except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
    24→            data["config_error"] = str(exc)
    25→            logger.debug("Skipping config injection into query payload: %s", exc)
    26→    try:
    27→        safe_write_text(
    28→            query_file, json.dumps(data, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    29→        )
    30→        print("  → query.json updated", file=sys.stderr)
    31→        return OutputResult(
    32→            ok=True,
    33→            status="written",
    34→            message=f"query payload written to {query_file}",
    35→        )
```

```
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [

   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
   201→    ]
   202→
   203→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   204→    return _build_merge_diff(
   205→        new_count=new_count,
   206→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   207→        reopened_count=reopened_count,
   208→        current_ids=current_ids,

   217→    )
```

```
1→"""Scorecard badge image generator — produces a visual health summary PNG."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→from importlib import metadata as importlib_metadata
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.dimensions import (
    12→    collapse_elegance_dimensions,
    13→    limit_scorecard_dimensions,
    14→    prepare_scorecard_dimensions,
    15→    resolve_scorecard_lang,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.meta import (
    18→    resolve_package_version,
    19→    resolve_project_name,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.theme import (
    22→    ACCENT,
    23→    BG,
    24→    BG_ROW_ALT,
    25→    BG_SCORE,
    26→    BG_TABLE,
    27→    BORDER,
    28→    DIM,
    29→    FRAME,
    30→    SCALE,
    31→    TEXT,
    32→    fmt_score,
    33→    load_font,
    34→    scale,
    35→    score_color,
    36→)
    37→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    38→from desloppify.state import get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    39→
    40→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    41→
    42→
    43→def generate_scorecard(state: dict, output_path: str | Path) -> Path:
    44→    """Render a landscape scorecard PNG from scan state. Returns the output path."""
    45→    image_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.Image")  # deferred: optional dependency
    46→    image_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("PIL.ImageDraw")  # deferred: optional dependency
    47→    scorecard_draw_mod = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard_parts.draw")  # deferred: depends on PIL
    48→
    49→    output_path = Path(output_path)
    50→
    51→    main_score = get_overall_score(state) or 0
    52→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state) or 0
    53→
    54→    project_name = resolve_project_name(PROJECT_ROOT)
    55→    package_version = resolve_package_version(
    56→        PROJECT_ROOT,
    57→        version_getter=importlib_metadata.version,
    58→        package_not_found_error=importlib_metadata.PackageNotFoundError,
    59→    )
    60→
    61→    # Layout — landscape (wide), File health first
    62→    active_dims = prepare_scorecard_dimensions(state)
    63→    row_count = len(active_dims)
    64→    row_h = scale(20)
    65→    width = scale(780)
    66→    divider_x = scale(260)
    67→    frame_inset = scale(5)
    68→
    69→    cols = 2
    70→    rows_per_col = (row_count + cols - 1) // cols
    71→    table_content_h = scale(14) + scale(4) + scale(6) + rows_per_col * row_h
    72→    content_h = max(table_content_h + scale(28), scale(150))
    73→    height = scale(12) + content_h
    74→
    75→    img = image_mod.new("RGB", (width, height), BG)
    76→    draw = image_draw_mod.Draw(img)
    77→
    78→    # Double frame
    79→    draw.rectangle((0, 0, width - 1, height - 1), outline=FRAME, width=scale(2))
    80→    draw.rectangle(
    81→        (frame_inset, frame_inset, width - frame_inset - 1, height - frame_inset - 1),
    82→        outline=BORDER,
    83→        width=1,
    84→    )
    85→
    86→    content_top = frame_inset + scale(1)
    87→    content_bot = height - frame_inset - scale(1)
    88→    content_mid_y = (content_top + content_bot) // 2
    89→
    90→    # Left panel: title + score + project name
    91→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_left_panel(
    92→        draw,
    93→        main_score,
    94→        strict_score,
    95→        project_name,
    96→        package_version,
    97→        lp_left=frame_inset + scale(11),
    98→        lp_right=divider_x - scale(11),
    99→        lp_top=content_top + scale(4),
   100→        lp_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   101→    )
   102→
   103→    # Vertical divider with ornament
   104→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_vert_rule_with_ornament(
   105→        draw,
   106→        divider_x,
   107→        content_top + scale(12),
   108→        content_bot - scale(12),
   109→        content_mid_y,
   110→        BORDER,
   111→        ACCENT,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    # Right panel: dimension table
   115→    scorecard_draw_mod.draw_right_panel(
   116→        draw,
   117→        active_dims,
   118→        row_h,
   119→        table_x1=divider_x + scale(11),
   120→        table_x2=width - frame_inset - scale(11),
   121→        table_top=content_top + scale(4),
   122→        table_bot=content_bot - scale(4),
   123→    )
   124→    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   125→    img.save(str(output_path), "PNG", optimize=True)
   126→    return output_path
   127→
   128→
   129→def get_badge_config(args, config: dict | None = None) -> tuple[Path | None, bool]:
   130→    """Resolve badge output path and whether badge generation is disabled.
   131→
   132→    Returns (output_path, disabled). Checks CLI args, then config, then env vars.
   133→    """
   134→    cfg = config or {}
   135→    disabled = getattr(args, "no_badge", False)
   136→    if not disabled:
   137→        disabled = not cfg.get("generate_scorecard", True)
   138→    if not disabled:
   139→        disabled = os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_NO_BADGE", "").lower() in (
   140→            "1",
   141→            "true",
   142→            "yes",
   143→        )
   144→    if disabled:
   145→        return None, True
   146→
   147→    path_str = (
   148→        getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
   149→        or cfg.get("badge_path")
   150→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH", "scorecard.png")
```

```
1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]
    60→
```

```
1→"""State-path and scan-gating helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import auto_detect_lang_name
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    10→
    11→
    12→def _sole_existing_lang_state_file() -> Path | None:
    13→    """Return the only existing language-specific state file, if unambiguous."""
    14→    state_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify"
    15→    if not state_dir.exists():
    16→        return None
    17→    candidates = sorted(path for path in state_dir.glob("state-*.json") if path.is_file())
    18→    if len(candidates) == 1:
    19→        return candidates[0]
    20→    return None
    21→
    22→
    23→def _allow_lang_state_fallback(args) -> bool:
    24→    """Whether command can safely fallback to the sole existing lang state file."""
    25→    # Scan should always honor detected/explicit language mapping to avoid cross-lang merges.
    26→    return getattr(args, "command", None) != "scan"
    27→
    28→
    29→def state_path(args) -> Path | None:
    30→    """Get state file path from args, or None for default."""
    31→    path_arg = getattr(args, "state", None)
    32→    if path_arg:
    33→        return Path(path_arg)
    34→    lang_name = getattr(args, "lang", None)
    35→    if not lang_name:
    36→        lang_name = auto_detect_lang_name(args)
    37→    if lang_name:
    38→        resolved = PROJECT_ROOT / ".desloppify" / f"state-{lang_name}.json"
    39→        if resolved.exists() or not _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    40→            return resolved
    41→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    42→        if fallback is not None:
    43→            return fallback
    44→        return resolved
    45→
    46→    if _allow_lang_state_fallback(args):
    47→        fallback = _sole_existing_lang_state_file()
    48→        if fallback is not None:
    49→            return fallback
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def require_completed_scan(state: dict) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True when the state contains at least one completed scan."""
    55→    has_completed_scan = bool(state.get("last_scan"))
    56→    if not has_completed_scan:
    57→        print(colorize("No scans yet. Run: desloppify scan", "yellow"))
    58→    return has_completed_scan
    59→
```

```
1→"""Runtime context helpers for command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class CommandRuntime:
    16→    """Explicit runtime dependencies shared by command handlers."""
    17→
    18→    config: dict[str, Any]
    19→    state: dict[str, Any]
    20→    state_path: Path | None
    21→
    22→
    23→def command_runtime(args) -> CommandRuntime:
    24→    """Return runtime context from explicit args.runtime or construct one."""
    25→    runtime = getattr(args, "runtime", None)
    26→    if isinstance(runtime, CommandRuntime):
    27→        return runtime
    28→
    29→    config = load_config()
    30→    state_file = state_path(args)
    31→    if isinstance(state_file, str):
    32→        state_file = Path(state_file)
    33→
    34→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    35→
    36→    return CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
    37→
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["CommandRuntime", "command_runtime"]
    40→
```

```
1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→) -> None:
    17→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps."""
    18→    if not steps:
    19→        return
    20→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    21→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    22→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    23→    if next_command:
    24→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    25→
    26→
    27→def print_replacement_groups(
    28→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    29→    *,
    30→    title: str,
    31→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    34→    if not groups:
    35→        return
    36→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    37→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    38→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    39→        for old, new in replacements:
    40→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    41→    print()
    42→
    43→
    44→def print_ranked_actions(actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3) -> bool:
    45→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    46→    ranked = sorted(
    47→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    48→        key=lambda action: (
    49→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    50→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    51→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    52→        ),
    53→    )
    54→    if not ranked:
    55→        return False
    56→    print(colorize("  Biggest things impacting score:", "cyan"))
    57→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    58→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    59→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    60→        command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
    61→        print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
    62→    return True
    63→
    64→
    65→__all__ = [
    66→    "print_agent_plan",
    67→    "print_ranked_actions",
    68→    "print_replacement_groups",
    69→]
    70→
```

```
1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]
    60→
```

```
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→
    11→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    12→
    13→
    14→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    15→    state: dict,
    16→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    17→    *,
    18→    attestation: str | None,
    19→) -> list[str]:
    20→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    21→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    22→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    23→            state,
    24→            pattern,
    25→            args.status,
    26→            args.note,
    27→            attestation=attestation,
    28→        )
    29→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    30→    return all_resolved
    31→
    32→
    33→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    34→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    35→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    36→        payload,
    37→        context="resolve query payload update",
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    42→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    43→    write_query(
    44→        {
    45→            "command": "resolve",
    46→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    47→            "status": context.status,
    48→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    49→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    50→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    51→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    52→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    53→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    54→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    55→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    56→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    57→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    58→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    59→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    60→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    61→        }
    62→    )
    63→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→
```

```
1→"""Payload and artifact helpers for scan command output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→import os
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_contracts import ScanQueryPayload
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_workflow import (
    11→    ScanMergeResult,
    12→    ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core.config import config_for_query
    16→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    17→from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
    18→from desloppify.state import open_scope_breakdown, score_snapshot
    19→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    20→
    21→
    22→def build_scan_query_payload(
    23→    state: dict[str, object],
    24→    config: dict[str, object],
    25→    profile: str,
    26→    diff: dict[str, object],
    27→    warnings: list[str],
    28→    narrative: dict[str, object],
    29→    merge: ScanMergeResult,
    30→    noise: ScanNoiseSnapshot,
    31→) -> ScanQueryPayload:
    32→    """Build the canonical query payload persisted after a scan."""
    33→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    34→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    35→    open_scope = (
    36→        open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    37→        if isinstance(findings, dict)
    38→        else None
    39→    )
    40→    return {
    41→        "command": "scan",
    42→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
    43→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
    44→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
    45→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    46→        "prev_overall_score": merge.prev_overall,
    47→        "prev_objective_score": merge.prev_objective,
    48→        "prev_strict_score": merge.prev_strict,
    49→        "prev_verified_strict_score": merge.prev_verified,
    50→        "profile": profile,
    51→        "noise_budget": noise.noise_budget,
    52→        "noise_global_budget": noise.global_noise_budget,
    53→        "hidden_by_detector": noise.hidden_by_detector,
    54→        "hidden_total": noise.hidden_total,
    55→        "diff": diff,
    56→        "stats": state["stats"],
    57→        "open_scope": open_scope,
    58→        "warnings": warnings,
    59→        "dimension_scores": state.get("dimension_scores"),
    60→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(state.get("dimension_scores", {})),
    61→        "subjective_integrity": state.get("subjective_integrity"),
    62→        "score_confidence": state.get("score_confidence"),
    63→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
    64→        "scan_coverage": state.get("scan_coverage"),
    65→        "zone_distribution": state.get("zone_distribution"),
    66→        "narrative": narrative,
    67→        "config": config_for_query(config),
    68→    }
    69→
    70→
    71→def _load_scorecard_helpers():
    72→    """Load scorecard helper callables lazily via importlib.
    73→
    74→    Deferred: scorecard depends on PIL (optional dependency).
    75→    """
    76→    try:
    77→        scorecard_module = importlib.import_module("desloppify.app.output.scorecard")
    78→    except ImportError:
    79→        return None, None
    80→    generate = getattr(scorecard_module, "generate_scorecard", None)
    81→    badge_config = getattr(scorecard_module, "get_badge_config", None)
    82→    return generate, badge_config
    83→
    84→
    85→def emit_scorecard_badge(
    86→    args, config: dict[str, object], state: dict[str, object]
    87→) -> tuple[Path | None, OutputResult]:
    88→    """Generate a scorecard image badge and print usage hints."""
    89→    generate_scorecard, get_badge_config = _load_scorecard_helpers()
    90→    if not callable(generate_scorecard) or not callable(get_badge_config):
    91→        explicit_badge_request = bool(
    92→            getattr(args, "badge_path", None)
    93→            or config.get("badge_path")
    94→            or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_BADGE_PATH")
    95→        )
    96→        if explicit_badge_request:
    97→            print(
    98→                colorize(
    99→                    "  Scorecard support not installed. Install with: pip install \"desloppify[scorecard]\"",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→            return None, OutputResult(
   104→                ok=False,
   105→                status="error",
   106→                message="scorecard support not installed",
   107→                error_kind="scorecard_dependency_missing",
   108→            )
   109→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   110→
   111→    try:
   112→        badge_path, disabled = get_badge_config(args, config)
   113→    except OSError as exc:
   114→        print(
   115→            colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not resolve scorecard badge path: {exc}", "yellow")
   116→        )
   117→        return None, OutputResult(
   118→            ok=False,
   119→            status="error",
   120→            message=str(exc),
   121→            error_kind="badge_path_resolution_error",
   122→        )
   123→    if disabled or not badge_path:
   124→        return None, OutputResult(ok=True, status="skipped", message="badge generation disabled")
   125→
   126→    try:
   127→        generate_scorecard(state, badge_path)
   128→    except (OSError, ImportError) as exc:
   129→        print(colorize(f"  ⚠ Could not generate scorecard badge: {exc}", "yellow"))
   130→        return None, OutputResult(
   131→            ok=False,
   132→            status="error",
   133→            message=str(exc),
   134→            error_kind="badge_generation_error",
   135→        )
   136→
   137→    try:
   138→        rel_path = str(badge_path.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT))
   139→    except ValueError:
   140→        rel_path = str(badge_path)
   141→
   142→    readme_has_badge = False
   143→    for readme_name in ("README.md", "readme.md", "README.MD"):
   144→        readme_path = PROJECT_ROOT / readme_name
   145→        if readme_path.exists():
   146→            try:
   147→                readme_has_badge = rel_path in readme_path.read_text(
   148→                    encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
   149→                )
   150→            except OSError:
   151→                readme_has_badge = False
   152→            break
   153→
   154→    if readme_has_badge:
   155→        print(
   156→            colorize(
   157→                f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}  (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)",
   158→                "dim",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→        return badge_path, OutputResult(
   162→            ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   163→        )
   164→
   165→    print(colorize(f"  Scorecard → {rel_path}", "dim"))
   166→    print(
   167→        colorize(
   168→            "  💡 Ask the user if they'd like to add it to their README with:",
   169→            "dim",
   170→        )
   171→    )
   172→    print(colorize(f'     <img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">', "dim"))
   173→    print(colorize("     (disable: --no-badge | move: --badge-path <path>)", "dim"))
   174→    return badge_path, OutputResult(
   175→        ok=True, status="written", message=f"scorecard badge written to {rel_path}"
   176→    )
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = ["build_scan_query_payload", "emit_scorecard_badge"]
   180→
```

```
    55→
    56→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    57→    """Print the scan header line."""
    58→    print(colorize(f"\nDesloppify Scan{lang_label}\n", "bold"))
    59→
    60→
    61→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:
    62→    """Print scan completion hint banner."""
    63→    lines = [
    64→        colorize("  Scan complete", "bold"),
    65→        colorize("  " + "─" * 50, "dim"),
    66→    ]
    67→    print("\n".join(lines))
    68→
    69→
    70→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    71→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    72→    if not effective_include_slow:
    73→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    74→    if noise.budget_warning:
    75→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    76→    if noise.hidden_total:
    77→        print(
    78→            colorize(
    79→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    80→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    81→                "dim",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    87→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    88→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    89→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    for entry in warnings:
    93→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    94→            continue
    95→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    96→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    97→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    98→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
    99→
   100→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   101→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   102→        if impact:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   104→        if remediation:
   105→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   106→
   107→
   108→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   109→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   110→    try:
   111→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   112→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   113→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   114→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   115→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   116→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   117→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   118→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   119→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   120→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   121→        runtime,
   122→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   123→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   124→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   125→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   126→    )
   127→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   128→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   129→        print(
   130→            colorize(
   131→                "  * Subjective reset "
   132→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   133→                "yellow",
   134→            )
   135→        )
   136→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   140→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   141→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   142→                "yellow",
   143→            )
   144→        )
   145→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   146→
   147→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   148→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   149→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   150→
   151→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   152→
   153→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   154→    show_score_delta(
   155→        runtime.state,
   156→        merge.prev_overall,
   157→        merge.prev_objective,
   158→        merge.prev_strict,
   159→        merge.prev_verified,
   160→    )
   161→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   162→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   163→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   164→
   165→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   166→
   167→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   168→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   169→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   170→
   171→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   172→        merge.diff,
   173→        runtime.state,
   174→        runtime.lang,
   175→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   176→    )
   177→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   178→
   179→    write_query(
   180→        build_scan_query_payload(
   181→            runtime.state,
   182→            runtime.config,
   183→            runtime.profile,
   184→            merge.diff,
   185→            warnings,
   186→            narrative,
   187→            merge,
   188→            noise,
   189→        ),
   190→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   191→    )
   192→
   193→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   194→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   195→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   196→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   197→        badge_path = badge_emit
   198→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   199→    auto_update_skill()
   200→
   201→
   202→__all__ = [
   203→    "cmd_scan",
   204→]
   205→
```
