```
1→"""Apply and reporting helpers for fix command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import subprocess
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    17→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    19→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    20→
    21→from .options import _COMMAND_POST_FIX
    22→
    23→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    24→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig
    25→    from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    26→
    27→
    28→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    29→    print(colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer.label}...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    30→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    31→    file_count = len(set(e["file"] for e in entries))
    32→    print(
    33→        colorize(
    34→            f"  Found {len(entries)} {fixer.label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim"
    35→        ),
    36→        file=sys.stderr,
    37→    )
    38→    return entries
    39→
    40→
    41→def _print_fix_summary(
    42→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    43→    results: list[dict],
    44→    total_items: int,
    45→    total_lines: int,
    46→    dry_run: bool,
    47→) -> None:
    48→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    49→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    53→            "bold",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    for r in results[:30]:
    57→        syms = ", ".join(r["removed"][:5])
    58→        if len(r["removed"]) > 5:
    59→            syms += f" (+{len(r['removed']) - 5})"
    60→        extra = f"  ({r['lines_removed']} lines)" if r.get("lines_removed") else ""
    61→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")
    62→    if len(results) > 30:
    63→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_and_report(
    67→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    68→    path: Path,
    69→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    70→    fixer_name: str,
    71→    entries: list[dict],
    72→    results: list[dict],
    73→    total_items: int,
    74→    lang: LangRun | None,
    75→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
    76→) -> None:
    77→    state_file = state_path(args)
    78→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    79→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    80→    resolved_ids = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, fixer.detector, fixer_name)
    81→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    82→
    83→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    84→    print(f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} findings in state")
    85→    if new.overall is not None and new.objective is not None and new.strict is not None:
    86→        overall_delta = new.overall - (prev.overall or 0)
    87→        delta_str = (
    88→            f" ({'+' if overall_delta > 0 else ''}{overall_delta:.1f})"
    89→            if overall_delta
    90→            else ""
    91→        )
    92→        print(
    93→            f"  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{delta_str}"
    94→            + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100", "dim")
    95→            + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100", "dim")
    96→        )
    97→    else:
    98→        print(colorize("  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    99→
   100→    if fixer.post_fix:
   101→        fixer.post_fix(path, state, prev.overall or 0, False, lang=lang)
   102→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   103→
   104→    if skip_reasons is None:
   105→        skip_reasons = {}
   106→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   107→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   108→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   109→        state,
   110→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
   111→    )
   112→    typecheck_cmd = getattr(lang, "typecheck_cmd", "")
   113→    if typecheck_cmd:
   114→        next_action = (
   115→            f"Run `{typecheck_cmd}` to verify, then `desloppify scan` to update state"
   116→        )
   117→    else:
   118→        next_action = "Run `desloppify scan` to update state"
   119→    write_query(
   120→        {
   121→            "command": "fix",
   122→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   123→            "files_fixed": len(results),
   124→            "items_fixed": total_items,
   125→            "findings_resolved": len(resolved_ids),
   126→            "overall_score": new.overall,
   127→            "objective_score": new.objective,
   128→            "strict_score": new.strict,
   129→            "prev_overall_score": prev.overall,
   130→            "prev_objective_score": prev.objective,
   131→            "prev_strict_score": prev.strict,
   132→            "skip_reasons": skip_reasons,
   133→            "next_action": next_action,
   134→            "narrative": narrative,
   135→        }
   136→    )
   137→    _print_fix_retro(
   138→        fixer_name, len(entries), total_items, len(resolved_ids), skip_reasons
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _report_dry_run(
   143→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   144→    fixer_name: str,
   145→    entries: list[dict],
   146→    results: list[dict],
   147→    total_items: int,
   148→) -> None:
   149→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   150→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   151→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   152→    state = runtime.state
   153→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   154→        state,
   155→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
   156→    )
   157→    write_query(
   158→        {
   159→            "command": "fix",
   160→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   161→            "dry_run": True,
   162→            "files_would_fix": len(results),
   163→            "items_would_fix": total_items,
   164→            "narrative": narrative,
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→    skipped = len(entries) - total_items
   168→    if skipped > 0:
   169→        print(colorize("\n  ── Review ──", "dim"))
   170→        print(
   171→            colorize(
   172→                f"  {total_items} of {len(entries)} entries would be fixed ({skipped} skipped).",
   173→                "dim",
   174→            )
   175→        )
   176→        for q in [
   177→            "Do the sample changes look correct? Any false positives?",
   178→            "Are the skipped items truly unfixable, or could the fixer be improved?",
   179→            "Ready to run without --dry-run? (git push first!)",
   180→        ]:
   181→            print(colorize(f"  - {q}", "dim"))
   182→
   183→
   184→def _resolve_fixer_results(
   185→    state: dict, results: list[dict], detector: str, fixer_name: str
   186→) -> list[str]:
   187→    resolved_ids = []
   188→    for r in results:
   189→        rfile = rel(r["file"])
   190→        for sym in r["removed"]:
   191→            fid = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{sym}"
   192→            if fid in state["findings"] and state["findings"][fid]["status"] == "open":
   193→                state["findings"][fid]["status"] = "fixed"
   194→                state["findings"][fid]["note"] = (
   195→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify fix {fixer_name}"
   196→                )
   197→                resolved_ids.append(fid)
   198→    return resolved_ids
   199→
   200→
   201→def _warn_uncommitted_changes() -> None:
   202→    try:
   203→        r = subprocess.run(
   204→            ["git", "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
   205→        )
   206→        if r.stdout.strip():
   207→            print(
   208→                colorize(
   209→                    "\n  ⚠ You have uncommitted changes. Consider running:", "yellow"
   210→                )
   211→            )
   212→            print(
   213→                colorize(
   214→                    "    git add -A && git commit -m 'pre-fix checkpoint' && git push",
   215→                    "yellow",
   216→                )
   217→            )
   218→            print(
   219→                colorize(
   220→                    "    This ensures you can revert if the fixer produces unexpected results.\n",
   221→                    "dim",
   222→                )
   223→            )
   224→    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   225→        return
   226→
   227→
   228→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
   229→    path: Path,
   230→    state: dict,
   231→    _prev_score: float,
   232→    dry_run: bool,
   233→    *,
   234→    lang: LangRun | None = None,
   235→) -> None:
   236→    if not lang or "unused-imports" not in getattr(lang, "fixers", {}):
   237→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
   238→        return
   239→
   240→    fixer = lang.fixers["unused-imports"]
   241→    print(colorize("\n  Running cascading import cleanup...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   242→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
   243→    if not entries:
   244→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   248→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
   249→        results = raw.entries
   250→    else:
   251→        results = raw
   252→
   253→    if not results:
   254→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   255→        return
   256→    n_removed = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
   257→    n_lines = sum(r["lines_removed"] for r in results)
   258→    print(
   259→        colorize(
   260→            f"  Cascade: removed {n_removed} now-orphaned imports "
   261→            f"from {len(results)} files ({n_lines} lines)",
   262→            "green",
   263→        )
   264→    )
   265→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   266→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   267→    )
   268→    if resolved:
   269→        print(f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import findings")
   270→
   271→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   272→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["dead-useeffect"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   273→
   274→_SKIP_REASON_LABELS = {
   275→    "rest_element": "has ...rest (removing changes rest contents)",
   276→    "array_destructuring": "array destructuring (positional — can't remove)",
   277→    "function_param": "function/callback parameter (use `fix unused-params` to prefix with _)",
   278→    "standalone_var_with_call": "standalone variable with function call (may have side effects)",
   279→    "no_destr_context": "destructuring member without context",
   280→    "out_of_range": "line out of range (stale data?)",
   281→    "other": "other patterns (needs manual review)",
   282→}
   283→
   284→
   285→def _print_fix_retro(
   286→    fixer_name: str,
   287→    detected: int,
   288→    fixed: int,
   289→    resolved: int,
   290→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   291→):
   292→    skipped = detected - fixed
   293→    print(colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim"))
   294→    print(
   295→        colorize(
   296→            f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} findings resolved)",
   297→            "dim",
   298→        )
   299→    )
   300→    if skip_reasons and skipped > 0:
   301→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim"))
   302→        for reason, count in sorted(skip_reasons.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
   303→            print(
   304→                colorize(
   305→                    f"    {count:4d}  {_SKIP_REASON_LABELS.get(reason, reason)}", "dim"
   306→                )
   307→            )
   308→        print()
   309→    checklist = [
   310→        "Run your language typecheck/build command — does it still build?",
   311→        "Spot-check a few changed files — do the edits look correct?",
   312→    ]
   313→    if skipped > 0 and not skip_reasons:
   314→        checklist.append(
   315→            f"{skipped} items were skipped. Should the fixer handle more patterns?"
   316→        )
   317→    checklist += [
   318→        "Run `desloppify scan` to update state. Did score improve as expected?",
   319→        "Are there cascading effects? (e.g., removing vars may orphan imports)",
   320→        "`git diff --stat` — review before committing. Anything surprising?",
   321→    ]
   322→    print(colorize("  Checklist:", "dim"))
   323→    for i, item in enumerate(checklist, 1):
   324→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. {item}", "dim"))
   325→
```

```
    37→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):
    38→    """Raised when review import CLI flags are mutually incompatible."""
    39→
    40→
    41→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    42→    *,
    43→    attested_external: bool,
    44→    allow_partial: bool,
    45→    override_enabled: bool,
    46→    override_attest: str | None,
    47→) -> None:
    48→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    49→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    50→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    51→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    52→        )
    53→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    54→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    55→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    56→        )
    57→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:

    59→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    60→        )
    61→    if override_enabled and (

    66→
    67→def _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    69→    raw_assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
    70→    keys: set[str] = set()
    71→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    72→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    73→        if normalized:
    74→            keys.add(normalized)
    75→    return keys
    76→
    77→
    78→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    79→    state: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    82→) -> int:
    83→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    84→    if not assessment_keys:
    85→        return 0
    86→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    87→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
    91→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
    92→    marked = 0
    93→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
    94→        payload = store.get(key)
    95→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    96→            continue
    97→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
    98→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
    99→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   100→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   101→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   102→        marked += 1
   103→    return marked
   104→
   105→
   106→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    *,
   109→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   110→) -> int:
   111→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   112→    if not assessment_keys:
   113→        return 0
   114→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   115→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   116→        return 0
   117→
   118→    cleared = 0
   119→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   120→        payload = store.get(key)
   121→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   122→            continue
   123→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   124→            cleared += 1
   125→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   126→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   127→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   128→    return cleared

   333→def do_validate_import(
   334→    import_file,
   335→    lang,
   336→    *,
   337→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   338→    attested_external: bool = False,
   339→    manual_override: bool = False,
   340→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   341→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   342→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   343→) -> None:
   344→    """Validate import payload/policy and print mode without mutating state."""
   345→    override_enabled, override_attest = import_helpers_mod.resolve_override_context(
   346→        manual_override=manual_override,
   347→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   348→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   349→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   350→    )
   351→    try:
   352→        _validate_import_flag_combos(
   353→            attested_external=attested_external,
   354→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   355→            override_enabled=override_enabled,
   356→            override_attest=override_attest,
   357→        )
   358→    except ImportFlagValidationError as exc:
   359→        print(colorize(f"  Error: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   360→        sys.exit(1)
   361→
   362→    try:
   363→        findings_data = import_helpers_mod.load_import_findings_data(
   364→            import_file,
   365→            lang_name=lang.name,
   366→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   367→            attested_external=attested_external,
   368→            manual_override=override_enabled,
   369→            manual_attest=override_attest,
   370→            assessment_override=assessment_override,
   371→            assessment_note=assessment_note,
   372→        )
   373→    except import_helpers_mod.ImportPayloadLoadError as exc:
   374→        import_helpers_mod.print_import_load_errors(
   375→            exc.errors,
   376→            import_file=str(import_file),
   377→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   378→        )
   379→        sys.exit(1)
   380→    assessment_policy = import_helpers_mod.assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   381→        findings_data
   382→    )
   383→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_mode_banner(
   384→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   385→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   386→    )
   387→    import_helpers_mod.print_assessment_policy_notice(
   388→        assessment_policy.to_dict(),
   389→        import_file=str(import_file),
   390→        colorize_fn=colorize,
   391→    )
   392→
   393→    findings_count = len(findings_data["findings"])
   394→    print(colorize("\n  Import payload validation passed.", "bold"))
   395→    print(colorize(f"  Findings parsed: {findings_count}", "dim"))
   396→    if assessment_policy.assessments_present:
   397→        count = int(assessment_policy.assessment_count)
   398→        print(colorize(f"  Assessment entries in payload: {count}", "dim"))
   399→    print(colorize("  No state changes were made (--validate-import).", "dim"))
   400→
   401→
   402→__all__ = [
   403→    "ImportFlagValidationError",
   404→    "do_import",
   405→    "do_validate_import",
   406→    "subjective_at_target_dimensions",
   407→]
   408→
```

```
1→"""Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import list_open_review_findings
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative

    14→
    15→
    16→def _word_set(text: str) -> set[str]:
    17→    words = "".join(ch.lower() if ch.isalnum() else " " for ch in text).split()
    18→    return {word for word in words if len(word) >= 3}
    19→
    20→
    21→def _summary_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    22→    left = _word_set(a)
    23→    right = _word_set(b)
    24→    if not left or not right:
    25→        return 0.0
    26→    overlap = len(left & right)
    27→    union = len(left | right)
    28→    return float(overlap) / float(union) if union else 0.0
    29→
    30→
    31→def _parse_holistic_identifier(finding_id: str) -> str:
    32→    parts = [part for part in str(finding_id).split("::") if part]
    33→    if len(parts) < 2:
    34→        return ""
    35→    candidate = parts[-2].strip()
    36→    if not candidate or candidate in {"holistic", "changed", "stale", "unreviewed"}:
    37→        return ""
    38→    return candidate
    39→
    40→
    41→def _related_files_set(finding: dict) -> set[str]:
    42→    related = finding.get("detail", {}).get("related_files", [])
    43→    if not isinstance(related, list):
    44→        return set()
    45→    return {str(path).strip() for path in related if str(path).strip()}
    46→
    47→
    48→def _same_issue_concept(
    49→    left: dict,
    50→    right: dict,
    51→    *,
    52→    similarity_threshold: float,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    left_detail = left.get("detail", {})
    55→    right_detail = right.get("detail", {})
    56→    if left_detail.get("dimension") != right_detail.get("dimension"):
    57→        return False
    58→
    59→    left_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(left.get("id", ""))
    60→    right_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(right.get("id", ""))
    61→    if left_identifier and right_identifier and left_identifier == right_identifier:
    62→        return True
    63→
    64→    left_summary = str(left.get("summary", "")).strip()
    65→    right_summary = str(right.get("summary", "")).strip()
    66→    similarity = _summary_similarity(left_summary, right_summary)
    67→    if similarity < similarity_threshold:
    68→        return False
    69→
    70→    left_files = _related_files_set(left)
    71→    right_files = _related_files_set(right)
    72→    if left_files and right_files and not (left_files & right_files):
    73→        return False
    74→    return True
    75→
    76→
    77→def _merge_finding_details(primary: dict, duplicate: dict) -> None:
    78→    primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
    79→    duplicate_detail = duplicate.get("detail", {})
    80→
    81→    for field in ("related_files", "evidence"):
    82→        merged: list[str] = []
    83→        seen: set[str] = set()
    84→        for source in (primary_detail.get(field), duplicate_detail.get(field)):
    85→            if not isinstance(source, list):
    86→                continue
    87→            for item in source:
    88→                value = str(item).strip()
    89→                if not value or value in seen:
    90→                    continue
    91→                seen.add(value)
    92→                merged.append(value)
    93→        if merged:
    94→            primary_detail[field] = merged
    95→
    96→    primary_suggestion = str(primary_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
    97→    duplicate_suggestion = str(duplicate_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
    98→    if len(duplicate_suggestion) > len(primary_suggestion):
    99→        primary_detail["suggestion"] = duplicate_suggestion
   100→
   101→    merged_from = primary_detail.get("merged_from")
   102→    if not isinstance(merged_from, list):
   103→        merged_from = []
   104→    duplicate_id = duplicate.get("id", "")
   105→    if duplicate_id and duplicate_id not in merged_from:
   106→        merged_from.append(duplicate_id)
   107→    if merged_from:
   108→        primary_detail["merged_from"] = merged_from
   109→
   110→
   111→def do_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   112→    """Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
   113→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   114→    state = runtime.state
   115→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   116→    narrative = compute_narrative(state, context=NarrativeContext(command="review"))
   117→    items = list_open_review_findings(state)
   118→
   119→    if not items:
   120→        print(colorize("\n  No review findings open.\n", "dim"))
   121→        return
   122→
   123→    try:
   124→        similarity = float(getattr(args, "similarity", 0.8))
   125→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   126→        similarity = 0.8
   127→    similarity = max(0.0, min(1.0, similarity))
   128→
   129→    open_holistic = [
   130→        finding
   131→        for finding in items
   132→        if finding.get("detector") == "review"
   133→        and finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic")
   134→    ]
   135→    if len(open_holistic) < 2:
   136→        print(colorize("\n  Not enough holistic review findings to merge.\n", "dim"))
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    consumed: set[str] = set()
   140→    merge_groups: list[list[dict]] = []
   141→    for candidate in open_holistic:
   142→        candidate_id = candidate.get("id", "")
   143→        if not candidate_id or candidate_id in consumed:
   144→            continue
   145→        group = [candidate]
   146→        consumed.add(candidate_id)
   147→        for other in open_holistic:
   148→            other_id = other.get("id", "")
   149→            if not other_id or other_id in consumed:
   150→                continue
   151→            if _same_issue_concept(
   152→                candidate,
   153→                other,
   154→                similarity_threshold=similarity,
   155→            ):
   156→                consumed.add(other_id)
   157→                group.append(other)
   158→        if len(group) > 1:
   159→            merge_groups.append(group)
   160→
   161→    if not merge_groups:
   162→        print(
   163→            colorize(
   164→                "\n  No duplicate issue concepts found at the current similarity threshold.\n",
   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   171→    timestamp = utc_now()
   172→    merged_pairs: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
   173→    for group in merge_groups:
   174→        ranked = sorted(
   175→            group,
   176→            key=lambda finding: (finding_weight(finding)[0], finding.get("id", "")),
   177→            reverse=True,
   178→        )
   179→        primary = ranked[0]
   180→        duplicates = ranked[1:]
   181→        merged_pairs.append((primary.get("id", ""), [d.get("id", "") for d in duplicates]))
   182→        if dry_run:
   183→            continue
   184→        for duplicate in duplicates:
   185→            _merge_finding_details(primary, duplicate)
   186→            duplicate["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   187→            duplicate["resolved_at"] = timestamp
   188→            duplicate["note"] = f"merged into {primary.get('id', '')}"
   189→            duplicate["resolution_attestation"] = {
   190→                "kind": "issue_merge",
   191→                "text": "Merged conceptually duplicate review finding",
   192→                "attested_at": timestamp,
   193→                "scan_verified": False,
   194→            }
   195→        primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
   196→        primary_detail["merged_at"] = timestamp
   197→
   198→    print(
   199→        colorize(
   200→            f"\n  Merge groups: {len(merge_groups)} | "
   201→            f"duplicate findings: {sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups)}",
   202→            "bold",
   203→        )
   204→    )
   205→    for index, (primary_id, duplicate_ids) in enumerate(merged_pairs, 1):
   206→        preview = ", ".join(duplicate_ids[:3])
   207→        if len(duplicate_ids) > 3:
   208→            preview = f"{preview}, +{len(duplicate_ids) - 3} more"
   209→        print(colorize(f"  [{index}] keep {primary_id}", "dim"))
   210→        print(colorize(f"      merge {preview}", "dim"))
   211→
   212→    if dry_run:
   213→        print(colorize("\n  Dry run only: no state changes written.\n", "yellow"))
   214→        return
   215→
   216→    save_state(state, state_file)
   217→    print(colorize("\n  State updated with merged issue groups.\n", "green"))
   218→    write_query(
   219→        {
   220→            "command": "review",
   221→            "action": "merge",
   222→            "groups": len(merge_groups),
   223→            "duplicates_merged": sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups),
   224→            "next_command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   225→            "narrative": narrative,
   226→        }
   227→    )
   228→
```

```
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}

   281→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   282→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   283→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   284→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   285→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   286→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   287→
   288→    findings = state["findings"]
   289→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   290→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   291→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   292→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   293→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   294→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   295→            raise ValueError(
   296→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   297→            )
   298→
   299→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   300→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   301→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   302→
   303→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   304→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   305→            raise ValueError(

   307→            )

   310→def json_default(obj: Any) -> Any:
   311→    """JSON serializer that handles known types and rejects unknowns."""
   312→    if isinstance(obj, set):
   313→        return sorted(obj)
   314→    if isinstance(obj, Path):
   315→        return str(obj).replace("\\", "/")
   316→    if hasattr(obj, "isoformat"):
   317→        return obj.isoformat()
   318→    raise TypeError(

   320→    )
```

```
1→"""Dimension and overall scoring aggregation helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import detector_stats_by_mode
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    10→    DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES,
    11→    DIMENSIONS,
    12→    DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME,
    13→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    14→    MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    15→    MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    16→    MIN_SAMPLE,
    17→    SCORING_MODES,
    18→    SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS,
    19→    SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION,
    20→    Dimension,
    21→    ScoreMode,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import (
    24→    append_subjective_dimensions,
    25→)
    26→
    27→
    28→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    33→    overall_score: float
    34→    objective_score: float
    35→    strict_score: float
    36→    verified_strict_score: float
    37→
    38→
    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
1→"""State persistence and migration routines."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→from typing import cast
    11→
    12→__all__ = [
    13→    "load_state",
    14→    "save_state",
    15→]
    16→
    17→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    CURRENT_VERSION,
    20→    STATE_FILE,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    empty_state,
    23→    ensure_state_defaults,
    24→    json_default,
    25→    validate_state_invariants,
    26→)
    27→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    28→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    34→    data = json.loads(path.read_text())
    35→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    36→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    37→    return data
    38→
    39→
    40→def _normalize_loaded_state(data: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    41→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    42→        raise ValueError("state file root must be a JSON object")
    43→    ensure_state_defaults(data)
    44→    normalized = cast(StateModel, data)
    45→    validate_state_invariants(normalized)
    46→    return normalized
    47→
    48→
    49→def load_state(path: Path | None = None) -> StateModel:
    50→    """Load state from disk, or return empty state on missing/corruption."""
    51→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    52→    if not state_path.exists():
    53→        return empty_state()
    54→
    55→    try:
    56→        data = _load_json(state_path)
    57→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as ex:
    58→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    59→        if backup.exists():
    60→            try:
    61→                backup_data = _load_json(backup)
    62→                print(
    63→                    f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.",
    64→                    file=sys.stderr,
    65→                )
    66→                return _normalize_loaded_state(backup_data)
    67→            except (
    68→                json.JSONDecodeError,
    69→                UnicodeDecodeError,
    70→                OSError,
    71→                ValueError,
    72→                TypeError,
    73→                AttributeError,
    74→            ) as backup_ex:
    75→                logger.debug("Backup state load failed from %s: %s", backup, backup_ex)
    76→
    77→        print(f"  ⚠ State file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    78→        rename_failed = False
    79→        try:
    80→            state_path.rename(state_path.with_suffix(".json.corrupted"))
    81→        except OSError as rename_ex:
    82→            rename_failed = True
    83→            logger.debug(
    84→                "Failed to rename corrupted state file %s: %s", state_path, rename_ex
    85→            )
    86→        if rename_failed:
    87→            logger.debug(
    88→                "Corrupted state file retained at original path: %s", state_path
    89→            )
    90→        return empty_state()
    91→
    92→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    93→    if version > CURRENT_VERSION:
    94→        print(
    95→            "  ⚠ State file version "
    96→            f"{version} is newer than supported ({CURRENT_VERSION}). "
    97→            "Some features may not work correctly.",
    98→            file=sys.stderr,
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    try:
   102→        return _normalize_loaded_state(data)
   103→    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as normalize_ex:
   104→        print(
   105→            f"  ⚠ State invariants invalid ({normalize_ex}). Starting fresh.",
   106→            file=sys.stderr,
   107→        )
   108→        return empty_state()
   109→
   110→
   111→def _coerce_integrity_target(value: object) -> float | None:
   112→    if not is_numeric(value):
   113→        return None
   114→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value)))
   115→
   116→
   117→def _resolve_integrity_target(
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    explicit_target: float | None,
   120→) -> float | None:
   121→    target = _coerce_integrity_target(explicit_target)
   122→    if target is not None:
   123→        return target
   124→
   125→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   126→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   127→        return None
   128→    return _coerce_integrity_target(integrity.get("target_score"))
   129→
   130→
   131→def save_state(
   132→    state: StateModel,
   133→    path: Path | None = None,
   134→    *,
   135→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   136→) -> None:
   137→    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
   138→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   139→    _recompute_stats(
   140→        state,
   141→        scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   142→        subjective_integrity_target=_resolve_integrity_target(
   143→            state,
   144→            subjective_integrity_target,
   145→        ),
   146→    )
   147→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   148→
   149→    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
   150→    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   151→
   152→    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   153→
   154→    if state_path.exists():
   155→        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   156→        try:
   157→            shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))
   158→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   159→            logger.debug(
   160→                "Failed to create state backup %s: %s",
   161→                state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak"),
   162→                backup_ex,
   163→            )
   164→
   165→    try:
   166→        safe_write_text(state_path, content)
   167→    except OSError as ex:
   168→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save state: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   169→        raise
   170→
```

```
1→"""State scoring, statistics, and suppression accounting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from copy import deepcopy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "suppression_metrics",
     9→]
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core._internal.coercions import coerce_confidence
    12→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import matches_target_score
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_findings
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, ensure_state_defaults
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import ScanCoverageRecord
    16→
    17→_EMPTY_COUNTERS = ("open", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "wontfix", "false_positive")
    18→_SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD = 2
    19→
    20→
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        status = finding["status"]
    45→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    46→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    47→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    48→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→
    50→    return counters, tier_stats
    51→
    52→
    53→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    54→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    55→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    56→    try:
    57→        score = float(raw)
    58→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    59→        score = 0.0
    60→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    61→
    62→
    63→def _subjective_target_matches(
    64→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    65→) -> list[str]:
    66→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    67→    matches = [
    68→        dimension
    69→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    70→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    71→    ]
    72→    return sorted(matches)
    73→
    74→
    75→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    77→    return {
    78→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    79→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),
    80→        "matched_count": 0,
    81→        "matched_dimensions": [],
    82→        "reset_dimensions": [],
    83→    }
    84→
    85→
    86→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    87→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    88→    *,
    89→    target: float,
    90→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    91→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    92→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    93→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    94→        subjective_assessments,
    95→        target=normalized_target,
    96→    )
    97→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
    98→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
    99→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   100→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→
```

```
    35→def show_tier_progress_table(by_tier: dict) -> None:

    42→        t_fp = ts.get("false_positive", 0)

   290→            "dim",

   292→    )
   293→    stale_keys = [
   294→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
   295→        for e in scorecard_subjective
   296→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
   297→    ]
   298→    has_open = False
   299→    if state is not None:
   300→        has_open = any(
   301→            f.get("status") == "open" and not f.get("suppressed")
   302→            for f in (state.get("findings") or {}).values()
   303→        )
   304→    if stale_keys and not has_open:
   305→        n = len(stale_keys)
   306→        dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
   307→        print(
   308→            colorize(
   309→                f"  {n} stale subjective dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   310→                f" — run `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}` to re-review",
   311→                "yellow",
   312→            )
   313→        )
   314→
   315→
   316→def show_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   317→    """Show dimension health table with dual scores and progress bars."""
   318→    print()
   319→    bar_len = 20
   320→    print(
   321→        colorize(
   322→            f"  {'Dimension':<22} {'Checks':>7}  {'Health':>6}  {'Strict':>6}  {'Bar':<{bar_len + 2}} {'Tier'}  {'Action'}",
   323→            "dim",
   324→        )
   325→    )
   326→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 86, "dim"))
   327→
   328→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   329→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   330→    review_issue_counts = _open_review_issue_counts(state)
   331→
   332→    _render_objective_dimensions(dim_scores, lowest_name=lowest_name, bar_len=bar_len)
   333→    _render_subjective_dimensions(
   334→        scorecard_subjective,
   335→        lowest_name=lowest_name,
   336→        bar_len=bar_len,
   337→        review_issue_counts=review_issue_counts,
   338→    )
   339→    _render_dimension_legend(scorecard_subjective, state=state)
   340→    print()
   341→
   342→
   343→def show_focus_suggestion(dim_scores: dict, state: dict) -> None:
   344→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   345→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   346→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   347→    if not lowest_name:
   348→        return
   349→
   350→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   351→    lowest_kind = None
   352→    lowest_score = 101.0
   353→    lowest_issues = 0
   354→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   355→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   356→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   357→            if ds:
   358→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   359→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   360→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   361→            break
   362→    else:
   363→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   364→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   365→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   366→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   367→                lowest_issues = 0
   368→                break
   369→
   370→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   371→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   372→            print(
   373→                colorize(
   374→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   375→                    "cyan",
   376→                )
   377→            )
   378→            print()
   379→            return
   380→
   381→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   382→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   383→        if target_dim:
   384→            impact = 0.0
   385→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   386→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   387→                    {
   388→                        k: {
   389→                            "score": v["score"],
   390→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   391→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   392→                        }
   393→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   394→                        if "score" in v
   395→                    },
   396→                    potentials,
   397→                    det,
   398→                    lowest_issues,
   399→                )
   400→                if impact > 0:
   401→                    break
   402→
   403→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   404→            print(
   405→                colorize(
   406→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   407→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   408→                    "cyan",
   409→                )
   410→            )
   411→            print()
   412→
   413→
   414→def show_subjective_followup(
   415→    state: dict, dim_scores: dict, *, target_strict_score: float
   416→) -> None:
   417→    """Show concrete subjective follow-up commands when applicable."""
   418→    if not dim_scores:
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   422→    if not subjective:
   423→        return
   424→
   425→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   426→        state,
   427→        subjective,
   428→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   429→        max_quality_items=3,
   430→        max_integrity_items=5,
   431→    )
   432→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   433→        print()
   434→
   435→
   436→def show_agent_plan(narrative: dict) -> None:
   437→    """Show concise action plan derived from narrative.actions."""
   438→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   439→    if not actions:
   440→        return
   441→
   442→    print(
   443→        colorize(

   445→            "yellow",
   446→        )
   447→    )
   448→    top = actions[0]
   449→    print(colorize(f"  Agent focus: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}", "cyan"))
   450→
   451→    if print_ranked_actions(actions):
   452→        print()
   453→
   454→
   455→def _collect_structural_areas(
   456→    state: dict,
   457→) -> list[tuple[str, list]] | None:
   458→    """Collect T3/T4 structural findings grouped by area.
   459→
   460→    Returns sorted area list, or None if insufficient data to display.
   461→    """
   462→    findings = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   463→        state.get("findings", {}), state.get("scan_path")
   464→    )
   465→
   466→    structural = [
   467→        f
   468→        for f in findings.values()
   469→        if f["tier"] in (3, 4) and f["status"] in ("open", "wontfix")
   470→    ]
   471→
   472→    if len(structural) < 5:
   473→        return None
   474→
   475→    areas: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
   476→    for f in structural:
   477→        area = get_area(str(f.get("file", "")))
   478→        areas[area].append(f)
   479→
   480→    if len(areas) < 2:
   481→        return None
   482→
   483→    return sorted(areas.items(), key=lambda x: -sum(f["tier"] for f in x[1]))
   484→
   485→
   486→def _build_area_rows(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> list[list[str]]:
   487→    """Build table rows from sorted area findings."""
   488→    rows = []
   489→    for area, area_findings in sorted_areas[:max_areas]:
   490→        t3 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 3)
   491→        t4 = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["tier"] == 4)
   492→        open_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "open")
   493→        debt_count = sum(1 for f in area_findings if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   494→        weight = sum(f["tier"] for f in area_findings)
   495→        rows.append(
   496→            [
   497→                area,
   498→                str(len(area_findings)),
   499→                f"T3:{t3} T4:{t4}",
   500→                str(open_count),
   501→                str(debt_count),
   502→                str(weight),
   503→            ]
   504→        )
   505→    return rows
   506→
   507→
   508→def _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas: list[tuple[str, list]], *, max_areas: int = 15) -> None:
   509→    """Print the overflow count and workflow instructions."""
   510→    remaining = len(sorted_areas) - max_areas
   511→    if remaining > 0:
   512→        print(colorize(f"\n  ... and {remaining} more areas", "dim"))
   513→
   514→    print(colorize("\n  Workflow:", "dim"))
   515→    print(colorize("    1. desloppify show <area> --status wontfix --top 50", "dim"))
   516→    print(
   517→        colorize(
   518→            "    2. Create tasks/<date>-<area-name>.md with decomposition plan", "dim"
   519→        )
   520→    )
   521→    print(
   522→        colorize("    3. Farm each task doc to a sub-agent for implementation", "dim")
   523→    )
   524→    print()
   525→
   526→
   527→def show_structural_areas(state: dict):
   528→    """Show structural debt grouped by area when T3/T4 debt is significant."""
   529→    sorted_areas = _collect_structural_areas(state)
   530→    if sorted_areas is None:
   531→        return
   532→
   533→    print(colorize("\n  ── Structural Debt by Area ──", "bold"))
   534→    print(
   535→        colorize(
   536→            "  Create a task doc for each area → farm to sub-agents for decomposition",
   537→            "dim",
   538→        )
   539→    )
   540→    print()
   541→
   542→    rows = _build_area_rows(sorted_areas)
   543→    print_table(
   544→        ["Area", "Items", "Tiers", "Open", "Debt", "Weight"], rows, [42, 6, 10, 5, 5, 7]
   545→    )
   546→
   547→    _render_area_workflow(sorted_areas)
   548→
   549→
   550→def show_review_summary(state: dict):
   551→    """Show review findings summary if any exist."""
   552→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   553→    review_open = [
   554→        f
   555→        for f in findings.values()
   556→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   557→    ]
   558→    if not review_open:
   559→        return
   560→    uninvestigated = sum(
   561→        1 for f in review_open if not f.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
   562→    )
   563→    parts = [f"{len(review_open)} finding{'s' if len(review_open) != 1 else ''} open"]
   564→    if uninvestigated:
   565→        parts.append(f"{uninvestigated} uninvestigated")
   566→    print(colorize(f"  Review: {', '.join(parts)} — `desloppify show review --status open`", "cyan"))
   567→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   568→    if "Test health" in dim_scores:
   569→        print(
   570→            colorize(
   571→                "  Test health tracks coverage + review; review findings track issues found.",
   572→                "dim",
   573→            )
   574→        )
   575→    print()
   576→
   577→
   578→__all__ = [
   579→    "print_open_scope_breakdown",
   580→    "print_scan_completeness",
   581→    "print_scan_metrics",
   582→    "score_summary_lines",
   583→    "show_agent_plan",
   584→    "show_dimension_table",
   585→    "show_focus_suggestion",
   586→    "show_ignore_summary",
   587→    "show_review_summary",
   588→    "show_structural_areas",
   589→    "show_subjective_followup",

   592→]
```

```
1→"""Summary-line rendering helpers for the status command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD, colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_summary_lines(
    10→    *,
    11→    overall_score: float | None,
    12→    objective_score: float | None,
    13→    strict_score: float | None,
    14→    verified_strict_score: float | None,
    15→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    16→    """Return formatted top-line score summary rows."""
    17→    if (
    18→        overall_score is not None
    19→        and objective_score is not None
    20→        and strict_score is not None
    21→        and verified_strict_score is not None
    22→    ):
    23→        return [
    24→            (
    25→                f"\n  Scores: overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 · "
    26→                f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 · "
    27→                f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 · "
    28→                f"verified {verified_strict_score:.1f}/100",
    29→                "bold",
    30→            ),
    31→            (
    32→                "  Score guide: overall (lenient) · objective (mechanical only) · "
    33→                "strict (penalizes wontfix) · verified (scan-confirmed only)",
    34→                "dim",
    35→            ),
    36→            ("  Focus on strict — it's your north star.", "dim"),
    37→        ]
    38→    return [
    39→        ("\n  Scores unavailable", "bold"),
    40→        ("  Run a full scan to compute overall/objective/strict scores.", "yellow"),
    41→    ]
    42→
    43→
    44→def print_scan_metrics(state: dict) -> None:
    45→    """Print aggregate codebase metrics from the last scan."""
    46→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    47→    total_files = sum(m.get("total_files", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    48→    total_loc = sum(m.get("total_loc", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    49→    total_dirs = sum(m.get("total_directories", 0) for m in metrics.values())
    50→    if total_files:
    51→        loc_str = f"{total_loc:,}" if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    52→        print(
    53→            colorize(
    54→                f"  {total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} dirs · "
    55→                f"Last scan: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    56→                "dim",
    57→            )
    58→        )
    59→        return
    60→    print(
    61→        colorize(
    62→            f"  Scans: {state.get('scan_count', 0)} | Last: {state.get('last_scan', 'never')}",
    63→            "dim",
    64→        )
    65→    )
    66→
    67→
    68→def print_scan_completeness(state: dict) -> None:
    69→    """Warn when one or more language scans were partial."""
    70→    completeness = state.get("scan_completeness", {})
    71→    incomplete = [lang for lang, status in completeness.items() if status != "full"]
    72→    if incomplete:
    73→        print(
    74→            colorize(
    75→                f"  * Incomplete scan ({', '.join(incomplete)} — slow phases skipped)",
    76→                "yellow",
    77→            )
    78→        )
    79→
    80→
    81→def print_open_scope_breakdown(state: dict) -> None:
    82→    """Print open counts with explicit in-scope/out-of-scope semantics."""
    83→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    counts = state_mod.open_scope_breakdown(findings, state.get("scan_path"))
    88→    print(
    89→        colorize(
    90→            "  "
    91→            f"open (in-scope): {counts['in_scope']} · "
    92→            f"open (out-of-scope carried): {counts['out_of_scope']} · "
    93→            f"open (global): {counts['global']}",
    94→            "dim",
    95→        )
    96→    )
    97→
    98→
    99→__all__ = [

   102→    "print_scan_metrics",

   104→]
```
