- `compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)` → recipe breakdown with mechanical/subjective fractions
- `compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issue_count)` → score delta for fixing issues
- `DimensionScore` TypedDict with: score, strict, checks, issues, tier, detectors

**DimensionScore structure:**
```python
class DimensionScore(TypedDict, total=False):
    score: float        # health score
    strict: float       # strict score (penalizes wontfix)
    checks: int         # number of checks performed
    issues: int         # open issue count
    tier: int           # 1-4
    detectors: dict     # by-detector breakdown
```

### 10. State Mutation Example: resolve command
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`

Pattern:
```python
def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    state_file = state_path(args)
    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)  # before mutation
    
    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)  # mutate
    
    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)  # save
    
    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)  # after scores
    # ... print movement, narrative, etc ...
    write_query({...})  # output
```

### 11. Atomic State Persistence
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/persistence.py` (lines 131-170)

```python
def save_state(
    state: StateModel,
    path: Path | None = None,
    *,
    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Recompute stats/score and save to disk atomically."""
    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    _recompute_stats(state, ...)  # compute all scores
    validate_state_invariants(state)
    
    state_path = path or STATE_FILE
    state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    content = json.dumps(state, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    
    if state_path.exists():
        backup = state_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
        shutil.copy2(str(state_path), str(backup))  # create backup
    
    safe_write_text(state_path, content)  # atomic write
```

---

**Summary:** Commands follow a consistent pattern: load state via `CommandRuntime` or direct `load_state()`, take a score snapshot, mutate state, save atomically, compute new snapshot, render output, write query payload. Post-fix hooks are dynamically attached via `_COMMAND_POST_FIX` dict. Score display uses reusable helpers from `status_parts/` and `scan_reporting_presentation.py` that can be imported and composed.

> DEVELOPER

## Task
Design the implementation for auto-refreshing and reporting scores after all score-changing operations (GitHub issue #160).

## Current State of Affairs

### Commands that already report score deltas well:
1. **`scan`** — `show_score_delta()` prints all 4 scores with colored deltas, strict target progress, dimension deltas
2. **`resolve`** — `_print_score_movement()` prints all 4 scores with deltas, wontfix debt gap
3. **`fix`** — prints overall delta inline, objective/strict/verified without deltas
4. **`ignore`** — prints all 4 scores (no deltas — just absolute values)

### Commands MISSING score delta reporting (the gap):
1. **`review --import`** (`do_import()` in `import_cmd.py`) — prints "Current scores: overall X · objective X · strict X" but NO deltas (no before-after comparison), no strict target progress
2. **`review --merge`** (`do_merge()` in `merge.py`) — saves state but prints NO scores at all
3. **`review --external-submit`** — delegates to `do_import()`, inherits its gaps
4. **`review --run-batches`** — delegates to `do_import()`, inherits its gaps

### Key insight: All commands already recompute scores
Every command that saves state calls `save_state()` which calls `_recompute_stats()`. So scores ARE always fresh after mutation. The only problem is they don't REPORT the delta to the user, and query.json doesn't always include scores.

## Existing Reusable Patterns

### Score snapshot pattern (used by resolve, fix, scan):
```python
prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)   # before mutation
# ... mutation ...
state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)    # after — scores recomputed by save_state
```
`ScoreSnapshot` is a NamedTuple with (overall, objective, strict, verified).

### Score delta display functions:
1. `show_score_delta(state, prev_overall, prev_objective, prev_strict, prev_verified)` in `scan_reporting_summary.py` — the most complete. Prints all 4 scores with colored deltas, wontfix gap, score legend on first scan.
2. `_print_score_movement(...)` in `resolve/render.py` — similar but with resolve-specific extras (review findings note, verified score explanation).
3. `_delta_suffix(delta)` in `resolve/render.py` — simple `(+1.7)` / `(-0.3)` suffix formatter.

### Strict target progress:
- `format_strict_target_progress(strict_target_dict)` in `status_parts/strict_target.py` — returns renderable lines
- `show_strict_target_progress(strict_target)` in `scan_reporting_summary.py` — prints those lines
- The `strict_target` dict comes from `narrative.compute_narrative()["strict_target"]`
- Target value comes from `target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)`

### Query payload with scores:
The scan command writes scores to query.json via `build_scan_query_payload()`. Other commands like resolve and fix also write scores to query.json.

## Design Approach

The user wants a centralized approach (option A from the issue). The key principle: **create one shared helper that any score-changing command can call after mutation**.

### Proposed: `print_score_update()` helper

A single function that:
1. Takes `prev` (ScoreSnapshot), `state` (current), optionally `config` (for target) and `command` name
2. Prints a compact score delta block: strict score with delta, target, and gap
3. The user specifically wants focus on **strict score** and **target score** — not all 4 scores

The user said: "the strict score and the target score should be constantly communicated"

### Where to add it

Create `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py` with:
- `print_score_update(prev, state, config=None, *, command=None)` — the main function
- Reuses `_delta_suffix()` logic from resolve/render.py
- Reuses `format_strict_target_progress()` from strict_target.py
- Includes scores in a standard format

### Touchpoints to wire in

1. **`review/import_cmd.py:do_import()`** — add `prev = score_snapshot(state)` before mutation, call `print_score_update()` after save_state
2. **`review/merge.py:do_merge()`** — add `prev = score_snapshot(state)` before merge, call `print_score_update()` after save_state  
3. **`review/external.py`** — flows through `do_import()`, so gets it for free
4. **`review/batch.py`** — flows through `do_import()`, so gets it for free

Also: replace the ad-hoc score printing in `import_helpers.py:print_review_import_scores_and_integrity()` with the new helper (it currently prints "Current scores" without deltas).

Also: add scores to query.json payloads where missing (review merge).

### Score data in query.json
Add `overall_score`, `strict_score`, `objective_score`, `verified_strict_score` to query payloads that currently omit them (review merge, review import).

## Implementation details

### The `print_score_update()` function

```python
def print_score_update(
    prev: ScoreSnapshot,
    state: dict,
    config: dict | None = None,
    *,
    command: str | None = None,
) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    """Print compact score delta block after a score-changing operation.
    
    Returns the new ScoreSnapshot for use in query payloads.
    """
    new = score_snapshot(state)
    # ... print strict score with delta ...
    # ... print target progress if config available ...
    return new
```

Output format should be compact and focused:
```
  Strict: 82.4 → 84.1 (+1.7)  |  Target: 95.0  |  Gap: 10.9
  Overall: 90.2 → 90.4 (+0.2)  Objective: 88.1 → 88.1
```

### Files to modify:
1. **NEW** `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py` — centralized helper
2. `desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py` — wire in before/after snapshot
3. `desloppify/app/commands/review/merge.py` — wire in before/after snapshot  
4. `desloppify/app/commands/review/import_helpers.py` — update `print_review_import_scores_and_integrity()` to use delta format

### Tests:
- Unit tests for `print_score_update()` output format
- Integration tests verifying review import/merge produce score deltas

```
1→"""Target-score normalization helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→
     7→
     8→def coerce_target_score(value: object, *, fallback: float = 95.0) -> float:
     9→    """Normalize target score-like values to a safe [0, 100] float."""
    10→    if is_numeric(fallback):
    11→        fallback_value = float(fallback)
    12→    else:
    13→        fallback_value = 95.0
    14→
    15→    if is_numeric(value):
    16→        parsed = float(value)
    17→    elif isinstance(value, str):
    18→        text = value.strip()
    19→        if not text:
    20→            parsed = fallback_value
    21→        else:
    22→            try:
    23→                parsed = float(text)
    24→            except ValueError:
    25→                parsed = fallback_value
    26→    else:
    27→        parsed = fallback_value
    28→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, parsed))
    29→
    30→
    31→def target_strict_score_from_config(
    32→    config: dict | None, *, fallback: float = 95.0
    33→) -> float:
    34→    """Read and normalize target strict score from config."""
    35→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    36→        raw = config.get("target_strict_score", fallback)
    37→    else:
    38→        raw = fallback
    39→    return coerce_target_score(raw, fallback=fallback)
    40→
    41→
```

```
    37→class ImportFlagValidationError(ValueError):
    38→    """Raised when review import CLI flags are mutually incompatible."""
    39→
    40→
    41→def _validate_import_flag_combos(
    42→    *,
    43→    attested_external: bool,
    44→    allow_partial: bool,
    45→    override_enabled: bool,
    46→    override_attest: str | None,
    47→) -> None:
    48→    """Fail fast on conflicting import flags to keep behavior explicit."""
    49→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
    50→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    51→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
    52→        )
    53→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
    54→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    55→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    56→        )
    57→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
    58→        raise ImportFlagValidationError(
    59→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial"
    60→        )
    61→    if override_enabled and (
    62→        not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip()
    63→    ):
    64→        raise ImportFlagValidationError("--manual-override requires --attest")
    65→
    66→
    67→def _imported_assessment_keys(findings_data: ReviewImportPayload) -> set[str]:
    68→    """Return normalized assessment dimension keys from payload."""
    69→    raw_assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
    70→    keys: set[str] = set()
    71→    for raw_key in raw_assessments:
    72→        normalized = normalize_dimension_name(str(raw_key))
    73→        if normalized:
    74→            keys.add(normalized)
    75→    return keys
    76→
    77→
    78→def _mark_manual_override_assessments_provisional(
    79→    state: dict,
    80→    *,
    81→    assessment_keys: set[str],
    82→) -> int:
    83→    """Mark imported manual override assessments as provisional until next scan."""
    84→    if not assessment_keys:
    85→        return 0
    86→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    87→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
    91→    expires_scan = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0) + 1
    92→    marked = 0
    93→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
    94→        payload = store.get(key)
    95→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    96→            continue
    97→        payload["source"] = "manual_override"
    98→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
    99→        payload["provisional_override"] = True
   100→        payload["provisional_until_scan"] = expires_scan
   101→        payload.pop("placeholder", None)
   102→        marked += 1
   103→    return marked
   104→
   105→
   106→def _clear_provisional_override_flags(
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    *,
   109→    assessment_keys: set[str],
   110→) -> int:
   111→    """Clear provisional override flags when trusted internal assessments replace them."""
   112→    if not assessment_keys:
   113→        return 0
   114→    store = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   115→    if not isinstance(store, dict):
   116→        return 0
   117→
   118→    cleared = 0
   119→    for key in sorted(assessment_keys):
   120→        payload = store.get(key)
   121→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   122→            continue
   123→        if payload.pop("provisional_override", None) is not None:
   124→            cleared += 1
   125→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   126→        if payload.get("source") == "manual_override":
   127→            payload["source"] = "holistic"
   128→    return cleared
   129→

   266→
   267→    lang_name = lang.name
   268→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
```

```
1→"""Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import list_open_review_findings
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative import NarrativeContext, compute_narrative
    13→from desloppify.state import save_state, utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→def _word_set(text: str) -> set[str]:
    17→    words = "".join(ch.lower() if ch.isalnum() else " " for ch in text).split()
    18→    return {word for word in words if len(word) >= 3}
    19→
    20→
    21→def _summary_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:
    22→    left = _word_set(a)
    23→    right = _word_set(b)
    24→    if not left or not right:
    25→        return 0.0
    26→    overlap = len(left & right)
    27→    union = len(left | right)
    28→    return float(overlap) / float(union) if union else 0.0
    29→
    30→
    31→def _parse_holistic_identifier(finding_id: str) -> str:
    32→    parts = [part for part in str(finding_id).split("::") if part]
    33→    if len(parts) < 2:
    34→        return ""
    35→    candidate = parts[-2].strip()
    36→    if not candidate or candidate in {"holistic", "changed", "stale", "unreviewed"}:
    37→        return ""
    38→    return candidate
    39→
    40→
    41→def _related_files_set(finding: dict) -> set[str]:
    42→    related = finding.get("detail", {}).get("related_files", [])
    43→    if not isinstance(related, list):
    44→        return set()
    45→    return {str(path).strip() for path in related if str(path).strip()}
    46→
    47→
    48→def _same_issue_concept(
    49→    left: dict,
    50→    right: dict,
    51→    *,
    52→    similarity_threshold: float,
    53→) -> bool:
    54→    left_detail = left.get("detail", {})
    55→    right_detail = right.get("detail", {})
    56→    if left_detail.get("dimension") != right_detail.get("dimension"):
    57→        return False
    58→
    59→    left_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(left.get("id", ""))
    60→    right_identifier = _parse_holistic_identifier(right.get("id", ""))
    61→    if left_identifier and right_identifier and left_identifier == right_identifier:
    62→        return True
    63→
    64→    left_summary = str(left.get("summary", "")).strip()
    65→    right_summary = str(right.get("summary", "")).strip()
    66→    similarity = _summary_similarity(left_summary, right_summary)
    67→    if similarity < similarity_threshold:
    68→        return False
    69→
    70→    left_files = _related_files_set(left)
    71→    right_files = _related_files_set(right)
    72→    if left_files and right_files and not (left_files & right_files):
    73→        return False
    74→    return True
    75→
    76→
    77→def _merge_finding_details(primary: dict, duplicate: dict) -> None:
    78→    primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
    79→    duplicate_detail = duplicate.get("detail", {})
    80→
    81→    for field in ("related_files", "evidence"):
    82→        merged: list[str] = []
    83→        seen: set[str] = set()
    84→        for source in (primary_detail.get(field), duplicate_detail.get(field)):
    85→            if not isinstance(source, list):
    86→                continue
    87→            for item in source:
    88→                value = str(item).strip()
    89→                if not value or value in seen:
    90→                    continue
    91→                seen.add(value)
    92→                merged.append(value)
    93→        if merged:
    94→            primary_detail[field] = merged
    95→
    96→    primary_suggestion = str(primary_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
    97→    duplicate_suggestion = str(duplicate_detail.get("suggestion", "")).strip()
    98→    if len(duplicate_suggestion) > len(primary_suggestion):
    99→        primary_detail["suggestion"] = duplicate_suggestion
   100→
   101→    merged_from = primary_detail.get("merged_from")
   102→    if not isinstance(merged_from, list):
   103→        merged_from = []
   104→    duplicate_id = duplicate.get("id", "")
   105→    if duplicate_id and duplicate_id not in merged_from:
   106→        merged_from.append(duplicate_id)
   107→    if merged_from:
   108→        primary_detail["merged_from"] = merged_from
   109→
   110→
   111→def do_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   112→    """Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings."""
   113→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   114→    state = runtime.state
   115→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   116→    narrative = compute_narrative(state, context=NarrativeContext(command="review"))
   117→    items = list_open_review_findings(state)
   118→
   119→    if not items:
   120→        print(colorize("\n  No review findings open.\n", "dim"))
   121→        return
   122→
   123→    try:
   124→        similarity = float(getattr(args, "similarity", 0.8))
   125→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   126→        similarity = 0.8
   127→    similarity = max(0.0, min(1.0, similarity))
   128→
   129→    open_holistic = [
   130→        finding
   131→        for finding in items
   132→        if finding.get("detector") == "review"
   133→        and finding.get("detail", {}).get("holistic")
   134→    ]
   135→    if len(open_holistic) < 2:
   136→        print(colorize("\n  Not enough holistic review findings to merge.\n", "dim"))
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    consumed: set[str] = set()
   140→    merge_groups: list[list[dict]] = []
   141→    for candidate in open_holistic:
   142→        candidate_id = candidate.get("id", "")
   143→        if not candidate_id or candidate_id in consumed:
   144→            continue
   145→        group = [candidate]
   146→        consumed.add(candidate_id)
   147→        for other in open_holistic:
   148→            other_id = other.get("id", "")
   149→            if not other_id or other_id in consumed:
   150→                continue
   151→            if _same_issue_concept(
   152→                candidate,
   153→                other,
   154→                similarity_threshold=similarity,
   155→            ):
   156→                consumed.add(other_id)
   157→                group.append(other)
   158→        if len(group) > 1:
   159→            merge_groups.append(group)
   160→
   161→    if not merge_groups:
   162→        print(
   163→            colorize(
   164→                "\n  No duplicate issue concepts found at the current similarity threshold.\n",
   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        )
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    dry_run = bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False))
   171→    timestamp = utc_now()
   172→    merged_pairs: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
   173→    for group in merge_groups:
   174→        ranked = sorted(
   175→            group,
   176→            key=lambda finding: (finding_weight(finding)[0], finding.get("id", "")),
   177→            reverse=True,
   178→        )
   179→        primary = ranked[0]
   180→        duplicates = ranked[1:]
   181→        merged_pairs.append((primary.get("id", ""), [d.get("id", "") for d in duplicates]))
   182→        if dry_run:
   183→            continue
   184→        for duplicate in duplicates:
   185→            _merge_finding_details(primary, duplicate)
   186→            duplicate["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   187→            duplicate["resolved_at"] = timestamp
   188→            duplicate["note"] = f"merged into {primary.get('id', '')}"
   189→            duplicate["resolution_attestation"] = {
   190→                "kind": "issue_merge",
   191→                "text": "Merged conceptually duplicate review finding",
   192→                "attested_at": timestamp,
   193→                "scan_verified": False,
   194→            }
   195→        primary_detail = primary.setdefault("detail", {})
   196→        primary_detail["merged_at"] = timestamp
   197→
   198→    print(
   199→        colorize(
   200→            f"\n  Merge groups: {len(merge_groups)} | "
   201→            f"duplicate findings: {sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups)}",
   202→            "bold",
   203→        )
   204→    )
   205→    for index, (primary_id, duplicate_ids) in enumerate(merged_pairs, 1):
   206→        preview = ", ".join(duplicate_ids[:3])
   207→        if len(duplicate_ids) > 3:
   208→            preview = f"{preview}, +{len(duplicate_ids) - 3} more"
   209→        print(colorize(f"  [{index}] keep {primary_id}", "dim"))
   210→        print(colorize(f"      merge {preview}", "dim"))
   211→
   212→    if dry_run:
   213→        print(colorize("\n  Dry run only: no state changes written.\n", "yellow"))
   214→        return
   215→
   216→    save_state(state, state_file)
   217→    print(colorize("\n  State updated with merged issue groups.\n", "green"))
   218→    write_query(
   219→        {
   220→            "command": "review",
   221→            "action": "merge",
   222→            "groups": len(merge_groups),
   223→            "duplicates_merged": sum(len(group) - 1 for group in merge_groups),
   224→            "next_command": "desloppify show review --status open",
   225→            "narrative": narrative,
   226→        }
   227→    )
   228→
```

```
   128→
   129→def _default_blind_packet_path() -> Path:
   130→    return get_project_root() / ".desloppify" / "review_packet_blind.json"
   131→
   132→
   133→def _is_sha256_hex(raw: object) -> bool:
   134→    return (
   135→        isinstance(raw, str)
   136→        and len(raw) == 64
   137→        and all(ch in "0123456789abcdefABCDEF" for ch in raw)
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def _hash_file_sha256(path: Path) -> str | None:
   142→    try:
   143→        data = path.read_bytes()
   144→    except OSError:
   145→        return None
   146→    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   147→
   148→
   149→def _resolve_packet_path(raw_path: object) -> Path | None:
   150→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str):
   151→        return None
   152→    text = raw_path.strip()
   153→    if not text:
   154→        return None
   155→    path = Path(text)
   156→    return path if path.is_absolute() else get_project_root() / path
   157→
   158→
   159→def _assessment_provenance_status(
   160→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   161→    *,
   162→    import_file: str,
   163→) -> AssessmentProvenanceModel:
   164→    """Evaluate whether assessments come from a trusted blind batch artifact."""
   165→    provenance = findings_data["provenance"]
   166→    if not provenance:
   167→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   168→            trusted=False,
   169→            reason="missing provenance metadata",
   170→            import_file=import_file,
   171→        )
   172→
   173→    kind = str(provenance.get("kind", "")).strip()
   174→    if kind != _BLIND_PROVENANCE_KIND:
   175→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   176→            trusted=False,
   177→            reason=f"unsupported provenance kind: {kind or '<missing>'}",
   178→            import_file=import_file,
   179→        )
   180→
   181→    if provenance.get("blind") is not True:
   182→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   183→            trusted=False,
   184→            reason="provenance is not marked blind=true",
   185→            import_file=import_file,
   186→        )
   187→
   188→    runner = str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip().lower()
   189→    if runner not in _SUPPORTED_BLIND_REVIEW_RUNNERS:
   190→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   191→            trusted=False,
   192→            reason=f"unsupported runner in provenance: {runner or '<missing>'}",
   193→            import_file=import_file,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→    packet_hash = provenance.get("packet_sha256")
   197→    if not _is_sha256_hex(packet_hash):
   198→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   199→            trusted=False,
   200→            reason="missing or invalid packet_sha256 in provenance",
   201→            import_file=import_file,
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    packet_path = _resolve_packet_path(provenance.get("packet_path"))
   205→    if packet_path is None:
   206→        packet_path = _default_blind_packet_path()
   207→    if not packet_path.exists():
   208→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   209→            trusted=False,
   210→            reason=f"blind packet not found: {packet_path}",
   211→            import_file=import_file,
   212→        )
   213→    observed_hash = _hash_file_sha256(packet_path)
   214→    if observed_hash is None:
   215→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   216→            trusted=False,
   217→            reason=f"unable to hash blind packet: {packet_path}",
   218→            import_file=import_file,
   219→        )
   220→    if observed_hash != packet_hash:
   221→        return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   222→            trusted=False,
   223→            reason=(
   224→                "blind packet hash mismatch "
   225→                f"(expected {packet_hash[:12]}..., got {observed_hash[:12]}...)"
   226→            ),
   227→            import_file=import_file,
   228→        )
   229→
   230→    return AssessmentProvenanceModel(
   231→        trusted=True,
   232→        reason="trusted blind subagent provenance",
   233→        runner=runner,
   234→        packet_path=str(packet_path),
   235→        packet_sha256=packet_hash,
   236→        import_file=import_file,
   237→    )
   238→

   240→def resolve_override_context(

   259→def _validate_attested_external_attestation(attest: str | None) -> str | None:
   260→    """Validate and normalize attestation text for attested external imports."""
   261→    if not isinstance(attest, str) or not attest.strip():
   262→        return None
   263→    text = attest.strip()
   264→    lowered = text.lower()
   265→    if all(phrase in lowered for phrase in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_REQUIRED_PHRASES):
   266→        return text
   267→    return None

   300→        print(
   301→            colorize_fn(

   303→                "dim",
   304→            ),

   306→        )
   307→        return
   308→
   309→    if (

   312→    ):
   313→        print(

   320→        print(colorize_fn(f"    `{import_cmd}`", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   321→        print(

   324→                "dim",

   328→        print(

   335→
   336→
   337→def _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   338→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   339→    *,
   340→    import_file: str,
   341→    attested_external: bool,
   342→    attested_attest: str | None,
   343→    manual_override: bool,
   344→    manual_attest: str | None,
   345→    trusted_assessment_source: bool,
   346→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None,
   347→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   348→    """Apply trust gating for assessment imports (findings import always allowed)."""
   349→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   350→    has_assessments = bool(assessments)
   351→    assessment_count = len(assessments) if has_assessments else 0
   352→    provenance_status = _assessment_provenance_status(
   353→        findings_data, import_file=import_file
   354→    )
   355→    policy = AssessmentImportPolicyModel(
   356→        assessments_present=has_assessments,
   357→        assessment_count=int(assessment_count),
   358→        trusted=False,
   359→        mode="none",
   360→        reason="",
   361→        provenance=provenance_status,
   362→    )
   363→
   364→    def _attach_policy(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   365→        normalized = dict(payload)
   366→        normalized[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   367→        return normalized
   368→
   369→    if not has_assessments:
   370→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   371→
   372→    if trusted_assessment_source:
   373→        policy = replace(
   374→            policy,
   375→            mode="trusted_internal",
   376→            trusted=True,
   377→            reason=(trusted_assessment_label or "trusted internal run-batches import"),
   378→        )
   379→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   380→
   381→    if attested_external:
   382→        normalized_attest = _validate_attested_external_attestation(attested_attest)
   383→        if normalized_attest is None:
   384→            return None, [
   385→                "--attested-external requires --attest containing both "
   386→                "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'"
   387→            ]
   388→        if provenance_status.trusted is not True:
   389→            return None, [
   390→                "--attested-external requires valid blind packet provenance "
   391→                f"(current status: {provenance_status.reason or 'untrusted provenance'})"
   392→            ]
   393→        runner = provenance_status.runner.strip().lower()
   394→        if runner not in _ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_RUNNERS:
   395→            return None, [
   396→                "--attested-external currently supports runner='claude' provenance only"
   397→            ]
   398→        policy = replace(
   399→            policy,
   400→            mode="attested_external",
   401→            trusted=True,
   402→            reason="attested external blind subagent provenance",
   403→            attest=normalized_attest,
   404→        )
   405→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   406→
   407→    if manual_override:
   408→        if not isinstance(manual_attest, str) or not manual_attest.strip():
   409→            return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   410→        policy = replace(
   411→            policy,
   412→            mode="manual_override",
   413→            reason="manual override attested by operator",
   414→            attest=manual_attest.strip(),
   415→        )
   416→        return _attach_policy(findings_data), []
   417→
   418→    policy = replace(policy, mode="findings_only")
   419→    if findings_data["provenance"]:
   420→        provenance_reason = provenance_status.reason.strip()
   421→        if provenance_status.trusted is True:
   422→            policy = replace(
   423→                policy,
   424→                reason=(
   425→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust even when provenance appears valid; "
   426→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   427→                ),
   428→            )
   429→        elif provenance_reason:
   430→            policy = replace(
   431→                policy,
   432→                reason=(
   433→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust "
   434→                    f"({provenance_reason}); run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   435→                ),
   436→            )
   437→        else:
   438→            policy = replace(
   439→                policy,
   440→                reason=(
   441→                    "external imports cannot self-attest trust; "
   442→                    "run review --run-batches to apply assessments automatically"
   443→                ),
   444→            )
   445→    else:
   446→        policy = replace(
   447→            policy,
   448→            reason="missing trusted run-batches source; imported findings only",
   449→        )
   450→    payload = dict(findings_data)
   451→    payload["assessments"] = {}
   452→    payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY] = policy.to_dict()
   453→    return payload, []
   454→
   455→
   456→def _has_non_empty_strings(items: object) -> bool:
   457→    """Return True when ``items`` is a list with at least one non-empty string."""
   458→    return isinstance(items, list) and any(
   459→        isinstance(item, str) and item.strip() for item in items
   460→    )
   461→
   462→
   463→def _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   464→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   465→    *,
   466→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   467→) -> list[str]:
   468→    """Validate strict holistic finding schema expected by issue import."""
   469→    findings = findings_data["findings"]
   470→
   471→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] = set()
   472→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip():
   473→        _, dimension_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   474→        allowed_dimensions = set(dimension_prompts)
   475→
   476→    errors: list[str] = []
   477→    for idx, entry in enumerate(findings):
   478→        _normalized: ReviewFindingPayload | None
   479→        _normalized, entry_errors = validate_review_finding_payload(
   480→            entry,
   481→            label=f"findings[{idx}]",
   482→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_dimensions or None,
   483→            allow_dismissed=True,
   484→        )
   485→        for message in entry_errors:
   486→            if (
   487→                "is not allowed" in message
   488→                and lang_name
   489→                and "dimension '" in message
   490→            ):
   491→                message = message.replace(
   492→                    "is not allowed",
   493→                    f"is not valid for language '{lang_name}'",
   494→                )
   495→            errors.append(message)
   496→    return errors
   497→
   498→
   499→def _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings: object) -> set[str]:
   500→    """Return dimensions with explicit improvement guidance in findings payload."""
   501→    if not isinstance(findings, list):
   502→        return set()
   503→    dims: set[str] = set()
   504→    for entry in findings:
   505→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   506→            continue
   507→        dim = entry.get("dimension")
   508→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   509→            continue
   510→        suggestion = entry.get("suggestion")
   511→        if isinstance(suggestion, str) and suggestion.strip():
   512→            dims.add(dim.strip())
   513→    return dims
   514→
   515→
   516→def _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(dimension_notes: object) -> set[str]:
   517→    """Return dimensions with concrete review evidence in dimension_notes payload."""
   518→    if not isinstance(dimension_notes, dict):
   519→        return set()
   520→    dims: set[str] = set()
   521→    for dim, note in dimension_notes.items():
   522→        if not isinstance(dim, str) or not dim.strip():
   523→            continue
   524→        if not isinstance(note, dict):
   525→            continue
   526→        if not _has_non_empty_strings(note.get("evidence")):
   527→            continue
   528→        dims.add(dim.strip())
   529→    return dims
   530→
   531→
   532→def _validate_assessment_feedback(
   533→    findings_data: ReviewImportPayload,
   534→) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   535→    """Return dimensions missing required feedback and required low-score findings."""
   536→    assessments = findings_data["assessments"]
   537→    if not assessments:
   538→        return [], []
   539→
   540→    finding_dims = _feedback_dimensions_from_findings(findings_data["findings"])
   541→    feedback_dims = set(finding_dims)
   542→    feedback_dims.update(
   543→        _feedback_dimensions_from_dimension_notes(findings_data["dimension_notes"])
   544→    )
   545→    missing_feedback: list[str] = []
   546→    missing_low_score_findings: list[str] = []
   547→    for dim_name, payload in assessments.items():
   548→        if not isinstance(dim_name, str) or not dim_name.strip():
   549→            continue
   550→        score = coerce_assessment_score(payload)
   551→        if score is None:
   552→            continue
   553→        if score_requires_dimension_finding(score) and dim_name not in finding_dims:
   554→            missing_low_score_findings.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   555→        if score_requires_explicit_feedback(score) and dim_name not in feedback_dims:
   556→            missing_feedback.append(f"{dim_name} ({score:.1f})")
   557→    return sorted(missing_feedback), sorted(missing_low_score_findings)
   558→
   559→
   560→def _parse_and_validate_import(
   561→    import_file: str,
   562→    *,
   563→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   564→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   565→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   566→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   567→    attested_external: bool = False,
   568→    manual_override: bool = False,
   569→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   570→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   571→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   572→) -> tuple[ReviewImportPayload | None, list[str]]:
   573→    """Parse and validate a review import file (pure function).
   574→
   575→    Returns ``(data, errors)`` where *data* is the normalized payload on
   576→    success, or ``None`` when errors prevent import.
   577→    """
   578→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   579→    if not findings_path.exists():
   580→        return None, [f"file not found: {import_file}"]
   581→    try:
   582→        findings_data = json.loads(findings_path.read_text())
   583→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   584→        return None, [f"error reading findings: {exc}"]
   585→
   586→    if isinstance(findings_data, list):
   587→        findings_data = {"findings": findings_data}
   588→
   589→    if not isinstance(findings_data, dict):
   590→        return None, ["findings file must contain a JSON array or object"]
   591→
   592→    if "findings" not in findings_data:
   593→        return None, ["findings object must contain a 'findings' key"]
   594→    normalized_findings_data, shape_errors = _normalize_import_payload_shape(
   595→        findings_data
   596→    )
   597→    if shape_errors:
   598→        return None, shape_errors
   599→    assert normalized_findings_data is not None
   600→
   601→    override_enabled, override_attest = resolve_override_context(
   602→        manual_override=manual_override,
   603→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   604→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   605→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   606→    )
   607→    if attested_external and override_enabled:
   608→        return None, [
   609→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --manual-override"
   610→        ]
   611→    if attested_external and allow_partial:
   612→        return None, [
   613→            "--attested-external cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   614→            "attested score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   615→        ]
   616→    if override_enabled and allow_partial:
   617→        return None, [
   618→            "--manual-override cannot be combined with --allow-partial; "
   619→            "manual score imports require fully valid findings payloads"
   620→        ]
   621→    findings_data, policy_errors = _apply_assessment_import_policy(
   622→        normalized_findings_data,
   623→        import_file=import_file,
   624→        attested_external=attested_external,
   625→        attested_attest=override_attest,
   626→        manual_override=override_enabled,
   627→        manual_attest=override_attest,
   628→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   629→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   630→    )
   631→    if policy_errors:
   632→        return None, policy_errors
   633→    assert findings_data is not None
   634→
   635→    missing_feedback, missing_low_score_findings = _validate_assessment_feedback(
   636→        findings_data
   637→    )
   638→    if missing_low_score_findings:
   639→        if override_enabled:
   640→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   641→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   642→            return findings_data, []
   643→        return None, [
   644→            f"assessments below {LOW_SCORE_FINDING_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include at "
   645→            "least one finding for that same dimension with a concrete suggestion. "
   646→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_low_score_findings)}"
   647→        ]
   648→
   649→    if missing_feedback:
   650→        if override_enabled:
   651→            if not isinstance(override_attest, str) or not override_attest.strip():
   652→                return None, ["--manual-override requires --attest"]
   653→            return findings_data, []
   654→        return None, [
   655→            f"assessments below {ASSESSMENT_FEEDBACK_THRESHOLD:.1f} must include explicit feedback "
   656→            "(finding with same dimension and non-empty suggestion, or "
   657→            "dimension_notes evidence for that dimension). "
   658→            f"Missing: {', '.join(missing_feedback)}"
   659→        ]
   660→
   661→    schema_errors = _validate_holistic_findings_schema(
   662→        findings_data,
   663→        lang_name=lang_name,
   664→    )
   665→    if schema_errors and not allow_partial:
   666→        visible_errors = schema_errors[:10]
   667→        remaining = len(schema_errors) - len(visible_errors)
   668→        errors = [
   669→            "findings schema validation failed for holistic import. "
   670→            "Fix payload or rerun with --allow-partial to continue."
   671→        ]
   672→        errors.extend(visible_errors)
   673→        if remaining > 0:
   674→            errors.append(f"... {remaining} additional schema error(s) omitted")
   675→        return None, errors
   676→
   677→    return findings_data, []
   678→
   679→
   680→def load_import_findings_data(
   681→    import_file: str,
   682→    *,
   683→    colorize_fn=None,
   684→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   685→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   686→    trusted_assessment_source: bool = False,
   687→    trusted_assessment_label: str | None = None,
   688→    attested_external: bool = False,
   689→    manual_override: bool = False,
   690→    manual_attest: str | None = None,
   691→    assessment_override: bool = False,
   692→    assessment_note: str | None = None,
   693→) -> ReviewImportPayload:
   694→    """Load and normalize review import payload to object format.
   695→
   696→    Raises ``ImportPayloadLoadError`` when validation fails.
   697→    """
   698→    data, errors = _parse_and_validate_import(
   699→        import_file,
   700→        lang_name=lang_name,
   701→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   702→        trusted_assessment_source=trusted_assessment_source,
   703→        trusted_assessment_label=trusted_assessment_label,
   704→        attested_external=attested_external,
   705→        manual_override=manual_override,
   706→        manual_attest=manual_attest,
   707→        assessment_override=assessment_override,
   708→        assessment_note=assessment_note,
   709→    )
   710→    if errors:
   711→        raise ImportPayloadLoadError(errors)
   712→    assert data is not None  # guaranteed when errors is empty
   713→    return data
   714→
   715→
   716→def print_import_load_errors(
   717→    errors: list[str],
   718→    *,
   719→    import_file: str,
   720→    colorize_fn,
   721→) -> None:
   722→    """Print import payload validation errors and actionable hints."""
   723→    for err in errors:
   724→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Error: {err}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   725→    _print_import_error_hints(errors, import_file=import_file, colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   726→
   727→
   728→def assessment_policy_from_payload(payload: ReviewImportPayload) -> AssessmentImportPolicy:
   729→    """Return parsed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   730→    policy = payload[_ASSESSMENT_POLICY_KEY]
   731→    if isinstance(policy, dict):
   732→        return policy
   733→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel().to_dict()
   734→
   735→
   736→def assessment_policy_model_from_payload(
   737→    payload: ReviewImportPayload,
   738→) -> AssessmentImportPolicyModel:
   739→    """Return typed assessment policy metadata from a loaded import payload."""
   740→    return AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(assessment_policy_from_payload(payload))
   741→
   742→
   743→def assessment_mode_label(policy: AssessmentImportPolicy) -> str:
   744→    """Return a user-facing label for the selected assessment import mode."""
   745→    mode = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy).mode.strip().lower()
   746→    return _ASSESSMENT_MODE_LABELS.get(mode, f"unknown ({mode or 'none'})")
   747→
   748→
   749→def print_assessment_mode_banner(
   750→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,
   751→    *,
   752→    colorize_fn,
   753→) -> None:
   754→    """Print the selected assessment import mode to make policy explicit."""
   755→    policy_model = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy)
   756→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   757→    assessments_present = bool(policy_model.assessments_present)
   758→    if not assessments_present and mode == "none":
   759→        return
   760→    style = "yellow" if mode in {"manual_override", "findings_only"} else "dim"
   761→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Assessment import mode: {assessment_mode_label(policy)}", style))
   762→
   763→
   764→def print_assessment_policy_notice(
   765→    policy: AssessmentImportPolicy,
   766→    *,
   767→    import_file: str,
   768→    colorize_fn,
   769→) -> None:
   770→    """Render trust/override status for assessment-bearing imports."""
   771→    policy_model = AssessmentImportPolicyModel.from_mapping(policy)
   772→    if not policy_model.assessments_present:
   773→        return
   774→    mode = policy_model.mode.strip().lower()
   775→    reason = policy_model.reason.strip()
   776→
   777→    if mode == "trusted":
   778→        packet_path = policy_model.provenance.packet_path.strip() or None
   779→        detail = f" · blind packet {packet_path}" if packet_path else ""
   780→        print(
   781→            colorize_fn(
   782→                f"  Assessment provenance: trusted blind batch artifact{detail}.",
   783→                "dim",
   784→            )
   785→        )
   786→        return
   787→
   788→    if mode == "trusted_internal":
   789→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   790→        reason_text = policy_model.reason.strip()
   791→        suffix = f" ({reason_text})" if reason_text else ""
   792→        print(
   793→            colorize_fn(
   794→                f"  Assessment updates applied: {count} dimension(s){suffix}.",
   795→                "dim",
   796→            )
   797→        )
   798→        return
   799→
   800→    if mode == "manual_override":
   801→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   802→        print(
   803→            colorize_fn(
   804→                f"  WARNING: applying {count} assessment update(s) via manual override from untrusted provenance.",
   805→                "yellow",
   806→            )
   807→        )
   808→        if reason:
   809→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   810→        return
   811→
   812→    if mode == "attested_external":
   813→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   814→        print(
   815→            colorize_fn(
   816→                f"  Assessment updates applied via attested external blind review: {count} dimension(s).",
   817→                "dim",
   818→            )
   819→        )
   820→        if reason:
   821→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   822→        return
   823→
   824→    if mode == "findings_only":
   825→        count = int(policy_model.assessment_count or 0)
   826→        print(
   827→            colorize_fn(
   828→                "  WARNING: untrusted assessment source detected. "
   829→                f"Imported findings only; skipped {count} assessment score update(s).",
   830→                "yellow",
   831→            )
   832→        )
   833→        if reason:
   834→            print(colorize_fn(f"  Reason: {reason}", "dim"))
   835→        print(
   836→            colorize_fn(
   837→                "  Assessment scores in state were left unchanged.",
   838→                "dim",
   839→            )
   840→        )
   841→        print(
   842→            colorize_fn(
   843→                "  Happy path: use `desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import`.",
   844→                "dim",
   845→            )
   846→        )
   847→        print(
   848→            colorize_fn(
   849→                "  If you intentionally want manual assessment import, rerun with "
   850→                f"`desloppify review --import {import_file} --manual-override --attest \"<why this is justified>\"`.",
   851→                "dim",
   852→            )
   853→        )
   854→        print(
   855→            colorize_fn(
   856→                "  Claude cloud path for durable scores: "
   857→                f"`desloppify review --import {import_file} --attested-external "
   858→                f"--attest \"{_ATTESTED_EXTERNAL_ATTEST_EXAMPLE}\"`",
   859→                "dim",
   860→            )
   861→        )
   862→
   863→
   864→def print_skipped_validation_details(diff: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   865→    """Print validation warnings for skipped imported findings."""
   866→    n_skipped = diff.get("skipped", 0)
   867→    if n_skipped <= 0:
   868→        return
   869→    print(
   870→        colorize_fn(
   871→            f"\n  \u26a0 {n_skipped} finding(s) skipped (validation errors):",
   872→            "yellow",
   873→        )
   874→    )
   875→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   876→        reasons = detail["missing"]
   877→        missing_fields = [r for r in reasons if not r.startswith("invalid ")]
   878→        validation_errors = [r for r in reasons if r.startswith("invalid ")]
   879→        parts = []
   880→        if missing_fields:
   881→            parts.append(f"missing {', '.join(missing_fields)}")
   882→        parts.extend(validation_errors)
   883→        print(
   884→            colorize_fn(
   885→                f"    #{detail['index']} ({detail['identifier']}): {'; '.join(parts)}",
   886→                "yellow",
   887→            )
   888→        )
   889→
   890→
   891→def print_assessments_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> None:
   892→    """Print holistic subjective assessment summary when present."""
   893→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
   894→    if not assessments:
   895→        return
   896→    parts = [
   897→        f"{key.replace('_', ' ')} {value['score']}"
   898→        for key, value in sorted(assessments.items())
   899→    ]
   900→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Assessments: {', '.join(parts)}", "bold"))
   901→
   902→
   903→def print_open_review_summary(state: dict[str, Any], *, colorize_fn) -> str:
   904→    """Print current open review finding count and return next command."""
   905→    open_review = [
   906→        finding
   907→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   908→        if finding["status"] == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
   909→    ]
   910→    if not open_review:
   911→        return "desloppify scan"
   912→    print(
   913→        colorize_fn(
   914→            f"\n  {len(open_review)} review issue{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total "
   915→            f"({len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open total)",
   916→            "bold",
   917→        )
   918→    )
   919→    print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue", "dim"))
   920→    return "desloppify show review --status open"
   921→
   922→
   923→def print_review_import_scores_and_integrity(
   924→    state: dict[str, Any],
   925→    config: dict[str, Any],
   926→    *,
   927→    state_mod,
   928→    target_strict_score_from_config_fn,
   929→    subjective_at_target_fn,
   930→    subjective_rerun_command_fn,
   931→    colorize_fn,
   932→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   933→    """Print score snapshot plus subjective integrity warnings."""
   934→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   935→    if scores.overall is not None and scores.objective is not None and scores.strict is not None:
   936→        print(
   937→            colorize_fn(
   938→                f"\n  Current scores: overall {scores.overall:.1f}/100 · "
   939→                f"objective {scores.objective:.1f}/100 · strict {scores.strict:.1f}/100",
   940→                "dim",
   941→            )
   942→        )
   943→
   944→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config_fn(config, fallback=95.0)
   945→    at_target = subjective_at_target_fn(
   946→        state,
   947→        state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   948→        target=target_strict,
   949→    )
   950→    if not at_target:
   951→        return []
   952→
   953→    command = subjective_rerun_command_fn(at_target, max_items=5)
   954→    count = len(at_target)
   955→    if count >= 2:
   956→        print(
   957→            colorize_fn(
   958→                "  WARNING: "
   959→                f"{count} subjective scores match the target score. "
   960→                "On the next scan, those dimensions will be reset to 0.0 by the anti-gaming safeguard "
   961→                f"unless you rerun and re-import objective reviews first: {command}",
   962→                "red",
   963→            )
   964→        )
   965→    else:
   966→        print(
   967→            colorize_fn(
   968→                "  WARNING: "
   969→                f"{count} subjective score matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   970→                f"Can you rerun it by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   971→                "yellow",
   972→            )
   973→        )
   974→    return at_target
   975→
```

```
1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    19→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    20→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    21→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    22→        return 0
    23→
    24→    streak = 0
    25→    for entry in reversed(history):
    26→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→            break
    28→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    29→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    30→            break
    31→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    32→            break
    33→        streak += 1
    34→    return streak
    35→
    36→
    37→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    38→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    39→    diff_parts = []
    40→    if diff["new"]:
    41→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    42→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    43→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    44→    if diff["reopened"]:
    45→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    46→    if diff_parts:
    47→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    48→    else:
    49→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    50→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    51→        print(
    52→            colorize(
    53→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    54→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    55→                "yellow",
    56→            )
    57→        )
    58→
    59→
    60→def show_score_delta(
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    prev_overall: float | None,
    63→    prev_objective: float | None,
    64→    prev_strict: float | None,
    65→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
    66→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
    67→):
    68→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
    69→    stats = state["stats"]
    70→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    71→
    72→    if (
    73→        new.overall is None
    74→        or new.objective is None
    75→        or new.strict is None
    76→        or new.verified is None
    77→    ):
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
    81→                "yellow",
    82→            )
    83→        )
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
    87→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = _format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
    88→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
    89→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = _format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
    90→    print(
    91→        "  Scores: "
    92→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
    93→        + colorize(
    94→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
    95→            objective_color,
    96→        )
    97→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
    98→        + colorize(
    99→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   100→            verified_color,
   101→        )
   102→    )
   103→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   104→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   105→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   106→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   107→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   108→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   109→        print(
   110→            colorize(
   111→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   112→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   113→                "yellow",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→
   117→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   118→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   119→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   120→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   121→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   122→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   123→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   124→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   125→
   126→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   127→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   128→        status = integrity.get("status")
   129→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   130→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   131→        if status == "penalized":
   132→            print(
   133→                colorize(
   134→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   135→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   136→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   137→                    "red",
   138→                )
   139→            )
   140→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   141→            if streak >= 2:
   142→                print(
   143→                    colorize(
   144→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   145→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   146→                        "yellow",
   147→                    )
   148→                )
   149→        elif status == "warn":
   150→            print(
   151→                colorize(
   152→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   153→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   154→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   155→                    "yellow",
   156→                )
   157→            )
   158→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   159→            if streak >= 2:
   160→                print(
   161→                    colorize(
   162→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   163→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   164→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   165→                        "yellow",
   166→                )
   167→            )
   168→
   169→
   170→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   171→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   172→    try:

   176→        if concerns:

   180→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   181→                    "cyan",
   182→                )
   183→            )
   184→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   185→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   186→
   187→
   188→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   189→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   190→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   191→    for message, style in lines:
   192→        print(colorize(message, style))
   193→    return target, gap
   194→

   196→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    12→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    13→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    14→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    15→        print(f"  {fid}")
    16→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    17→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    18→
    19→
    20→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    *,
    23→    status: str,
    24→    resolved_count: int,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    27→        return
    28→    wontfix_count = sum(
    29→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    30→    )
    31→    actionable = sum(
    32→        1
    33→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    34→        if finding["status"]
    35→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    36→    )
    37→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    38→    print(
    39→        colorize(
    40→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    41→            "yellow",
    42→        )
    43→    )
    44→    print(
    45→        colorize(
    46→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    47→            "dim",
    48→        )
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    53→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    54→        return ""
    55→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    56→
    57→
    58→def _print_score_movement(
    59→    *,
    60→    status: str,
    61→    prev_overall: float | None,
    62→    prev_objective: float | None,
    63→    prev_strict: float | None,
    64→    prev_verified: float | None,
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    67→) -> None:
    68→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    69→    if (
    70→        new.overall is None
    71→        or new.objective is None
    72→        or new.strict is None
    73→        or new.verified is None
    74→    ):
    75→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if status == "wontfix":
    94→        strict_gap = round(new.overall - new.strict, 1)
    95→        if strict_gap > 0:
    96→            print(
    97→                colorize(
    98→                    f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    99→                    f"Current gap: overall {new.overall:.1f} vs strict {new.strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
   100→                    "yellow",
   101→                )
   102→            )
   103→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
   104→        print(
   105→            colorize(
   106→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
   107→                "yellow",
   108→            )
   109→        )
   110→        print(
   111→            colorize(
   112→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
   113→                "dim",
   114→            )
   115→        )
   116→    elif status == "fixed":
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
   120→                "yellow",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→
   125→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   126→    *,
   127→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   128→    state: dict,
   129→    all_resolved: list[str],
   130→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   131→) -> None:
   132→    has_review = any(
   133→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   134→        for fid in all_resolved
   135→    )
   136→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   137→        return
   138→
   139→    stale_dims = sorted(
   140→        dim
   141→        for dim in {
   142→            str(
   143→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   144→            ).strip()
   145→            for fid in all_resolved
   146→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   147→        }
   148→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   149→    )
   150→    if not stale_dims:
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   154→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   155→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        )
   161→    )
   162→    print(
   163→        colorize(
   164→            "  Next subjective step: "
   165→            + f"`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(stale_dims)}`",
   166→            "dim",
   167→        )
   168→    )
   169→
   170→
   171→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   172→    remaining = sum(
   173→        1
   174→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   175→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   176→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   177→    )
   178→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   179→    if remaining > 0:
   180→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   181→        print(
   182→            colorize(
   183→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   184→                "dim",
   185→            )
   186→        )
   187→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   188→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   189→    print()
   190→    return next_command
   191→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→
```
