1→"""Apply and reporting helpers for fix command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import subprocess
     7→import sys
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
    10→
    11→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    15→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    17→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    18→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    19→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    20→
    21→from .options import _COMMAND_POST_FIX
    22→
    23→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    24→    from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixerConfig
    25→    from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun
    26→
    27→
    28→def _detect(fixer: FixerConfig, path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    29→    print(colorize(f"\nDetecting {fixer.label}...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    30→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
    31→    file_count = len(set(e["file"] for e in entries))
    32→    print(
    33→        colorize(
    34→            f"  Found {len(entries)} {fixer.label} across {file_count} files\n", "dim"
    35→        ),
    36→        file=sys.stderr,
    37→    )
    38→    return entries
    39→
    40→
    41→def _print_fix_summary(
    42→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    43→    results: list[dict],
    44→    total_items: int,
    45→    total_lines: int,
    46→    dry_run: bool,
    47→) -> None:
    48→    verb = fixer.dry_verb if dry_run else fixer.verb
    49→    lines_str = f" ({total_lines} lines)" if total_lines else ""
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            f"\n  {verb} {total_items} {fixer.label} across {len(results)} files{lines_str}\n",
    53→            "bold",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    for r in results[:30]:
    57→        syms = ", ".join(r["removed"][:5])
    58→        if len(r["removed"]) > 5:
    59→            syms += f" (+{len(r['removed']) - 5})"
    60→        extra = f"  ({r['lines_removed']} lines)" if r.get("lines_removed") else ""
    61→        print(f"  {rel(r['file'])}{extra}  →  {syms}")
    62→    if len(results) > 30:
    63→        print(f"  ... and {len(results) - 30} more files")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _apply_and_report(
    67→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    68→    path: Path,
    69→    fixer: FixerConfig,
    70→    fixer_name: str,
    71→    entries: list[dict],
    72→    results: list[dict],
    73→    total_items: int,
    74→    lang: LangRun | None,
    75→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
    76→) -> None:
    77→    state_file = state_path(args)
    78→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    79→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    80→    resolved_ids = _resolve_fixer_results(state, results, fixer.detector, fixer_name)
    81→    state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    82→
    83→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    84→    print(f"\n  Auto-resolved {len(resolved_ids)} findings in state")
    85→    if new.overall is not None and new.objective is not None and new.strict is not None:
    86→        overall_delta = new.overall - (prev.overall or 0)
    87→        delta_str = (
    88→            f" ({'+' if overall_delta > 0 else ''}{overall_delta:.1f})"
    89→            if overall_delta
    90→            else ""
    91→        )
    92→        print(
    93→            f"  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{delta_str}"
    94→            + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100", "dim")
    95→            + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100", "dim")
    96→        )
    97→    else:
    98→        print(colorize("  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    99→
   100→    if fixer.post_fix:
   101→        fixer.post_fix(path, state, prev.overall or 0, False, lang=lang)
   102→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
   103→
   104→    if skip_reasons is None:
   105→        skip_reasons = {}
   106→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   107→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   108→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   109→        state,
   110→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
   111→    )
   112→    typecheck_cmd = getattr(lang, "typecheck_cmd", "")
   113→    if typecheck_cmd:
   114→        next_action = (
   115→            f"Run `{typecheck_cmd}` to verify, then `desloppify scan` to update state"
   116→        )
   117→    else:
   118→        next_action = "Run `desloppify scan` to update state"
   119→    write_query(
   120→        {
   121→            "command": "fix",
   122→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   123→            "files_fixed": len(results),
   124→            "items_fixed": total_items,
   125→            "findings_resolved": len(resolved_ids),
   126→            "overall_score": new.overall,
   127→            "objective_score": new.objective,
   128→            "strict_score": new.strict,
   129→            "prev_overall_score": prev.overall,
   130→            "prev_objective_score": prev.objective,
   131→            "prev_strict_score": prev.strict,
   132→            "skip_reasons": skip_reasons,
   133→            "next_action": next_action,
   134→            "narrative": narrative,
   135→        }
   136→    )
   137→    _print_fix_retro(
   138→        fixer_name, len(entries), total_items, len(resolved_ids), skip_reasons
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→def _report_dry_run(
   143→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   144→    fixer_name: str,
   145→    entries: list[dict],
   146→    results: list[dict],
   147→    total_items: int,
   148→) -> None:
   149→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   150→    fix_lang = resolve_lang(args)
   151→    fix_lang_name = fix_lang.name if fix_lang else None
   152→    state = runtime.state
   153→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   154→        state,
   155→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=fix_lang_name, command="fix"),
   156→    )
   157→    write_query(
   158→        {
   159→            "command": "fix",
   160→            "fixer": fixer_name,
   161→            "dry_run": True,
   162→            "files_would_fix": len(results),
   163→            "items_would_fix": total_items,
   164→            "narrative": narrative,
   165→        }
   166→    )
   167→    skipped = len(entries) - total_items
   168→    if skipped > 0:
   169→        print(colorize("\n  ── Review ──", "dim"))
   170→        print(
   171→            colorize(
   172→                f"  {total_items} of {len(entries)} entries would be fixed ({skipped} skipped).",
   173→                "dim",
   174→            )
   175→        )
   176→        for q in [
   177→            "Do the sample changes look correct? Any false positives?",
   178→            "Are the skipped items truly unfixable, or could the fixer be improved?",
   179→            "Ready to run without --dry-run? (git push first!)",
   180→        ]:
   181→            print(colorize(f"  - {q}", "dim"))
   182→
   183→
   184→def _resolve_fixer_results(
   185→    state: dict, results: list[dict], detector: str, fixer_name: str
   186→) -> list[str]:
   187→    resolved_ids = []
   188→    for r in results:
   189→        rfile = rel(r["file"])
   190→        for sym in r["removed"]:
   191→            fid = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{sym}"
   192→            if fid in state["findings"] and state["findings"][fid]["status"] == "open":
   193→                state["findings"][fid]["status"] = "fixed"
   194→                state["findings"][fid]["note"] = (
   195→                    f"auto-fixed by desloppify fix {fixer_name}"
   196→                )
   197→                resolved_ids.append(fid)
   198→    return resolved_ids
   199→
   200→
   201→def _warn_uncommitted_changes() -> None:
   202→    try:
   203→        r = subprocess.run(
   204→            ["git", "status", "--porcelain"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
   205→        )
   206→        if r.stdout.strip():
   207→            print(
   208→                colorize(
   209→                    "\n  ⚠ You have uncommitted changes. Consider running:", "yellow"
   210→                )
   211→            )
   212→            print(
   213→                colorize(
   214→                    "    git add -A && git commit -m 'pre-fix checkpoint' && git push",
   215→                    "yellow",
   216→                )
   217→            )
   218→            print(
   219→                colorize(
   220→                    "    This ensures you can revert if the fixer produces unexpected results.\n",
   221→                    "dim",
   222→                )
   223→            )
   224→    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   225→        return
   226→
   227→
   228→def _cascade_unused_import_cleanup(
   229→    path: Path,
   230→    state: dict,
   231→    _prev_score: float,
   232→    dry_run: bool,
   233→    *,
   234→    lang: LangRun | None = None,
   235→) -> None:
   236→    if not lang or "unused-imports" not in getattr(lang, "fixers", {}):
   237→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no unused-imports fixer for this language", "dim"))
   238→        return
   239→
   240→    fixer = lang.fixers["unused-imports"]
   241→    print(colorize("\n  Running cascading import cleanup...", "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
   242→    entries = fixer.detect(path)
   243→    if not entries:
   244→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   245→        return
   246→
   247→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
   248→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
   249→        results = raw.entries
   250→    else:
   251→        results = raw
   252→
   253→    if not results:
   254→        print(colorize("  Cascade: no orphaned imports found", "dim"))
   255→        return
   256→    n_removed = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
   257→    n_lines = sum(r["lines_removed"] for r in results)
   258→    print(
   259→        colorize(
   260→            f"  Cascade: removed {n_removed} now-orphaned imports "
   261→            f"from {len(results)} files ({n_lines} lines)",
   262→            "green",
   263→        )
   264→    )
   265→    resolved = _resolve_fixer_results(
   266→        state, results, fixer.detector, "cascade-unused-imports"
   267→    )
   268→    if resolved:
   269→        print(f"  Cascade: auto-resolved {len(resolved)} import findings")
   270→
   271→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["debug-logs"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   272→_COMMAND_POST_FIX["dead-useeffect"] = _cascade_unused_import_cleanup
   273→
   274→_SKIP_REASON_LABELS = {
   275→    "rest_element": "has ...rest (removing changes rest contents)",
   276→    "array_destructuring": "array destructuring (positional — can't remove)",
   277→    "function_param": "function/callback parameter (use `fix unused-params` to prefix with _)",
   278→    "standalone_var_with_call": "standalone variable with function call (may have side effects)",
   279→    "no_destr_context": "destructuring member without context",
   280→    "out_of_range": "line out of range (stale data?)",
   281→    "other": "other patterns (needs manual review)",
   282→}
   283→
   284→
   285→def _print_fix_retro(
   286→    fixer_name: str,
   287→    detected: int,
   288→    fixed: int,
   289→    resolved: int,
   290→    skip_reasons: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   291→):
   292→    skipped = detected - fixed
   293→    print(colorize("\n  ── Post-fix check ──", "dim"))
   294→    print(
   295→        colorize(
   296→            f"  Fixed {fixed}/{detected} ({skipped} skipped, {resolved} findings resolved)",
   297→            "dim",
   298→        )
   299→    )
   300→    if skip_reasons and skipped > 0:
   301→        print(colorize(f"\n  Skip reasons ({skipped} total):", "dim"))
   302→        for reason, count in sorted(skip_reasons.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):
   303→            print(
   304→                colorize(
   305→                    f"    {count:4d}  {_SKIP_REASON_LABELS.get(reason, reason)}", "dim"
   306→                )
   307→            )
   308→        print()
   309→    checklist = [
   310→        "Run your language typecheck/build command — does it still build?",
   311→        "Spot-check a few changed files — do the edits look correct?",
   312→    ]
   313→    if skipped > 0 and not skip_reasons:
   314→        checklist.append(
   315→            f"{skipped} items were skipped. Should the fixer handle more patterns?"
   316→        )
   317→    checklist += [
   318→        "Run `desloppify scan` to update state. Did score improve as expected?",
   319→        "Are there cascading effects? (e.g., removing vars may orphan imports)",
   320→        "`git diff --stat` — review before committing. Anything surprising?",
   321→    ]
   322→    print(colorize("  Checklist:", "dim"))
   323→    for i, item in enumerate(checklist, 1):
   324→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. {item}", "dim"))
   325→

1→"""Shared strict-target progress rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def format_strict_target_progress(
     7→    strict_target: dict | None,
     8→) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, str]], float | None, float | None]:
     9→    """Return display lines and numeric values for strict target progress."""
    10→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    11→    if not isinstance(strict_target, dict):
    12→        return lines, None, None
    13→
    14→    warning = strict_target.get("warning")
    15→    if isinstance(warning, str) and warning.strip():
    16→        lines.append((f"  * {warning.strip()}", "yellow"))
    17→
    18→    target = strict_target.get("target")
    19→    current = strict_target.get("current")
    20→    gap = strict_target.get("gap")
    21→    state = strict_target.get("state")
    22→    if not isinstance(target, int | float):
    23→        return lines, None, None
    24→    target_f = float(target)
    25→
    26→    if not isinstance(current, int | float):
    27→        lines.append(
    28→            (
    29→                f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · current strict score unavailable",
    30→                "dim",
    31→            )
    32→        )
    33→        return lines, target_f, None
    34→
    35→    current_f = float(current)
    36→    gap_f = (
    37→        float(gap) if isinstance(gap, int | float) else round(target_f - current_f, 1)
    38→    )
    39→    if state == "below":
    40→        lines.append((
    41→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({gap_f:.1f} below target)",
    42→            "yellow",
    43→        ))
    44→    elif state == "above":
    45→        lines.append((
    46→            f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · currently {current_f:.1f}/100 ({abs(gap_f):.1f} above target)",
    47→            "green",
    48→        ))
    49→    else:
    50→        lines.append((f"  Strict target: {target_f:.1f}/100 · on target", "green"))
    51→
    52→    return lines, target_f, gap_f
    53→

1→"""Shared helpers for scan command orchestration."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify import languages as lang_api
     9→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    10→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS, read_file_text
    11→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→
    15→
    16→def _audit_excluded_dirs(
    17→    exclusions: tuple[str, ...],
    18→    scanned_files: list[str],
    19→    project_root: Path,
    20→) -> list[dict]:
    21→    """Check if any --exclude directory has zero references from scanned code.
    22→
    23→    Returns findings for directories that appear stale (no file references them).
    24→    """
    25→    if not exclusions:
    26→        return []
    27→
    28→    candidate_dirs = [
    29→        ex_dir
    30→        for ex_dir in exclusions
    31→        if ex_dir not in DEFAULT_EXCLUSIONS and (project_root / ex_dir).is_dir()
    32→    ]
    33→    if not candidate_dirs:
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    # Read each scanned file once and mark any exclusions observed in content.
    37→    unresolved = set(candidate_dirs)
    38→    for filepath in scanned_files:
    39→        if not unresolved:
    40→            break
    41→        abs_path = (
    42→            filepath if Path(filepath).is_absolute() else str(project_root / filepath)
    43→        )
    44→        content = read_file_text(abs_path)
    45→        if content is None:
    46→            logger.debug(
    47→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while auditing exclusions", filepath
    48→            )
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        matched = {ex_dir for ex_dir in unresolved if ex_dir in content}
    52→        if matched:
    53→            unresolved.difference_update(matched)
    54→
    55→    stale_findings = []
    56→    for ex_dir in candidate_dirs:
    57→        if ex_dir not in unresolved:
    58→            continue
    59→        stale_findings.append(
    60→            state_mod.make_finding(
    61→                "stale_exclude",
    62→                ex_dir,
    63→                ex_dir,
    64→                tier=4,
    65→                confidence="low",
    66→                summary=(
    67→                    f"Excluded directory '{ex_dir}' has 0 references from scanned code — "
    68→                    "may be stale"
    69→                ),
    70→                detail={"directory": ex_dir, "references": 0},
    71→            )
    72→        )
    73→    return stale_findings
    74→
    75→
    76→def _collect_codebase_metrics(lang, path: Path) -> dict | None:
    77→    """Collect LOC/file/directory counts for the configured language."""
    78→    if not lang or not lang.file_finder:
    79→        return None
    80→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    81→    total_loc = 0
    82→    dirs = set()
    83→    for filepath in files:
    84→        try:
    85→            total_loc += len(Path(filepath).read_text().splitlines())
    86→            dirs.add(str(Path(filepath).parent))
    87→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
    88→            logger.debug(
    89→                "Skipping unreadable file %s while collecting scan metrics: %s",
    90→                filepath,
    91→                exc,
    92→            )
    93→    return {
    94→        "total_files": len(files),
    95→        "total_loc": total_loc,
    96→        "total_directories": len(dirs),
    97→    }
    98→
    99→
   100→def _resolve_scan_profile(profile: str | None, lang) -> str:
   101→    """Resolve effective scan profile from CLI and language defaults."""
   102→    if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   103→        return profile
   104→    default_profile = getattr(lang, "default_scan_profile", "full") if lang else "full"
   105→    if default_profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"}:
   106→        return default_profile
   107→    return "full"
   108→
   109→
   110→def _effective_include_slow(include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> bool:
   111→    """Determine whether slow phases should run for this profile."""
   112→    return include_slow and profile != "ci"
   113→
   114→
   115→def _format_hidden_by_detector(hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> str:
   116→    """Format hidden findings counts for terminal output."""
   117→    return ", ".join(f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items())
   118→
   119→
   120→def _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   121→    args, lang, path: Path, metrics: dict | None
   122→) -> None:
   123→    """Warn when user explicitly selected a language but scan found zero files."""
   124→    explicit_lang = getattr(args, "lang", None)
   125→    if not explicit_lang or not lang or not metrics:
   126→        return
   127→    if metrics.get("total_files", 0) > 0:
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    suggestion = " Omit `--lang` to auto-detect."
   131→    root = path if path.is_dir() else path.parent
   132→    try:
   133→        detected = lang_api.auto_detect_lang(root)
   134→    except ValueError as exc:
   135→        detected = None
   136→        logger.debug(
   137→            "Auto-detect failed while warning for explicit lang on %s: %s", root, exc
   138→        )
   139→    else:
   140→        if detected and detected != lang.name:
   141→            suggestion = (
   142→                f" Detected `{detected}` for this path — use `--lang {detected}` "
   143→                "or omit `--lang`."
   144→            )
   145→
   146→    print(
   147→        colorize(
   148→            f"  ⚠ No {lang.name} source files found under `{path}`.{suggestion}",
   149→            "yellow",
   150→        )
   151→    )
   152→
   153→
   154→def _format_delta(value: float, prev: float | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
   155→    """Return (delta_str, color) for a score change."""
   156→    delta = value - prev if prev is not None else 0
   157→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta != 0 else ""
   158→    color = "green" if delta > 0 else ("red" if delta < 0 else "dim")
   159→    return delta_str, color
   160→
   161→
   162→__all__ = [
   163→    "_audit_excluded_dirs",
   164→    "_collect_codebase_metrics",
   165→    "_effective_include_slow",
   166→    "_format_delta",
   167→    "_format_hidden_by_detector",
   168→    "_resolve_scan_profile",
   169→    "_warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files",
   170→]
   171→

     5→import argparse

     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path

    16→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod

    20→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    21→from .render import (

    23→    _print_resolve_summary,
    24→    _print_score_movement,
    25→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,

    29→    ResolveQueryContext,
    30→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,

    32→    _show_attestation_requirement,

    36→
    37→
    38→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    39→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    40→    try:
    41→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    42→    except OSError as exc:
    43→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    44→        sys.exit(1)
    45→
    46→
    47→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    48→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    49→    try:
    50→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    51→    except OSError as exc:
    52→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    53→        sys.exit(1)
    54→
    55→
    56→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    57→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    58→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    59→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    60→
    61→    state_file = state_path(args)
    62→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    63→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    64→        state,
    65→        args,
    66→        attestation=attestation,
    67→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    68→    )
    69→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    70→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    71→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    72→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    73→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    77→    if not all_resolved:
    78→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    79→        print(
    80→            colorize(
    81→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    82→                "yellow",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→        return
    86→
    87→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    88→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
    89→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    90→        state,
    91→        status=args.status,
    92→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
    93→    )
    94→    has_review_findings = any(
    95→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
    96→        for fid in all_resolved
    97→    )
    98→    _print_score_movement(
    99→        status=args.status,
   100→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   101→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   102→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   103→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   104→        state=state,
   105→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   106→    )
   107→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   108→        args=args,
   109→        state=state,
   110→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   111→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   112→    )
   113→
   114→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   115→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   116→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   117→        state,
   118→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   119→    )
   120→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   121→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   122→
   123→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   124→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   125→        ResolveQueryContext(
   126→            patterns=args.patterns,
   127→            status=args.status,
   128→            resolved=all_resolved,
   129→            next_command=next_command,
   130→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   131→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   132→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   133→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   134→            attestation=attestation,
   135→            narrative=narrative,
   136→            state=state,
   137→        )
   138→    )
   139→
   140→
   141→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   142→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   143→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   144→    if not _validate_attestation(attestation):
   145→        _show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   146→        sys.exit(1)
   147→
   148→    state_file = state_path(args)
   149→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   150→
   151→    config = command_runtime(args).config
   152→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   153→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   154→
   155→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   156→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   157→        {
   158→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   159→            "command": "ignore",
   160→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   161→            "attestation": attestation,
   162→            "affected": removed,
   163→        }
   164→    )
   165→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   166→
   167→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   168→    if removed:
   169→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   170→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   171→    if (
   172→        scores.overall is not None
   173→        and scores.objective is not None
   174→        and scores.strict is not None
   175→        and scores.verified is not None
   176→    ):
   177→        print(
   178→            f"  Scores: overall {scores.overall:.1f}/100"
   179→            + colorize(f"  objective: {scores.objective:.1f}/100", "dim")
   180→            + colorize(f"  strict: {scores.strict:.1f}/100", "dim")
   181→            + colorize(f"  verified: {scores.verified:.1f}/100", "dim")
   182→        )
   183→    print()
   184→
   185→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   186→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   187→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   188→        state,
   189→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="ignore"),
   190→    )
   191→    write_query(
   192→        {
   193→            "command": "ignore",
   194→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   195→            "removed": removed,
   196→            "overall_score": scores.overall,

   199→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
   200→            "attestation": attestation,
   201→            "narrative": narrative,
   202→        }
   203→    )

1→"""Query output helpers for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.query_paths import query_file_path
    10→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    11→from desloppify.core.query import write_query as _core_write_query
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    16→class QueryWriter:
    17→    """Command-level query artifact writer with explicit failure policy."""
    18→
    19→    query_file: Path
    20→
    21→    def write(self, data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    22→        return _core_write_query(data, query_file=self.query_file)
    23→
    24→    def write_best_effort(self, data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    25→        result = self.write(data)
    26→        if not result.ok:
    27→            detail = result.message or "unknown write failure"
    28→            print(
    29→                colorize(
    30→                    f"  Warning: {context} succeeded but query artifact was not written ({detail}).",
    31→                    "yellow",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→        return result
    36→
    37→
    38→def query_writer() -> QueryWriter:
    39→    """Construct a writer bound to the active runtime query path."""
    40→    return QueryWriter(query_file=query_file_path())
    41→
    42→
    43→def write_query(data: dict) -> OutputResult:
    44→    """Write structured query output using default best-effort policy."""
    45→    return write_query_best_effort(data, context="query payload update")
    46→
    47→
    48→def write_query_best_effort(data: dict, *, context: str) -> OutputResult:
    49→    """Write query payload and emit a contextual warning on failure."""
    50→    return query_writer().write_best_effort(data, context=context)
    51→
    52→
    53→__all__ = [
    54→    "QueryWriter",
    55→    "query_file_path",
    56→    "query_writer",
    57→    "write_query",
    58→    "write_query_best_effort",
    59→]

    36→def _runtime_project_root() -> Path:
    37→    if isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path):

    58→def _utc_now() -> datetime:

    60→
    61→
    62→def _iso_seconds(dt: datetime) -> str:
    63→    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _parse_iso(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    67→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    68→        return None
    69→    try:
    70→        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    71→    except ValueError:
    72→        return None
    73→    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    74→        return dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
    75→    return dt.astimezone(UTC)
    76→
    77→
    78→def _split_dimensions(raw: object) -> list[str] | None:
    79→    if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    80→        return None
    81→    values = [value.strip() for value in raw.split(",")]
    82→    cleaned = [value for value in values if value]
    83→    return cleaned or None
    84→

    87→    return f"ext_{runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()}_{secrets.token_hex(4)}"

    98→def _validate_session_id_or_exit(session_id: str) -> None:
    99→    if not session_id.strip():
   100→        print(colorize("  Error: --session-id is required.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   101→        sys.exit(2)
   102→    invalid_chars = {"/", "\\", ".."}
   103→    if any(part in session_id for part in invalid_chars):
   104→        print(colorize("  Error: invalid --session-id value.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   105→        sys.exit(2)

   107→
   108→def _load_json_object(path: Path, *, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   109→    if not path.exists():
   110→        print(colorize(f"  Error: {label} not found: {path}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   111→        sys.exit(1)
   112→    try:
   113→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   114→    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   115→        print(colorize(f"  Error: failed reading {label}: {exc}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   116→        sys.exit(1)
   117→    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   118→        print(
   119→            colorize(f"  Error: {label} must contain a JSON object.", "red"),
   120→            file=sys.stderr,
   121→        )
   122→        sys.exit(1)
   123→    return payload

   125→
   126→def _session_payload_or_exit(session_id: str) -> tuple[Path, dict[str, Any]]:
   127→    _validate_session_id_or_exit(session_id)

   130→    payload_id = str(payload.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   131→    if payload_id != session_id:
   132→        print(

   135→                "red",
   136→            ),
   137→            file=sys.stderr,
   138→        )
   139→        sys.exit(1)
   140→    return path, payload

   143→def _prepare_packet_snapshot(
   144→    args,

   149→) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path, Path]:
   150→    """Prepare holistic review packet and persist immutable+blind snapshots."""
   151→    path = Path(getattr(args, "path", ".") or ".")
   152→    dimensions = _split_dimensions(getattr(args, "dimensions", None))
   153→    retrospective = bool(getattr(args, "retrospective", False))

   244→            "7. Do not include provenance metadata (CLI injects canonical provenance).",

   246→        ]
   247→    )

   250→def do_external_start(args, state, lang, *, config: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None:
   251→    """Start an external review session with CLI-issued provenance context."""
   252→    config = config or {}
   253→    runner = str(getattr(args, "external_runner", "claude")).strip().lower()
   254→    if runner not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   255→        print(

   258→                "red",

   260→            file=sys.stderr,
   261→        )
   262→        sys.exit(2)
   263→    ttl_hours = int(getattr(args, "session_ttl_hours", 24) or 0)
   264→    if ttl_hours <= 0:
   265→        print(colorize("  Error: --session-ttl-hours must be > 0.", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   266→        sys.exit(2)
   267→

   270→        state,
   271→        lang,
   272→        config=config,
   273→    )

   277→            colorize(f"  Error: failed to hash blind packet: {blind_path}", "red"),
   278→            file=sys.stderr,
   279→        )
   280→        sys.exit(1)
   281→
   282→    now = _utc_now()
   283→    expires = now + timedelta(hours=ttl_hours)
   284→    session_id = _session_id()
   285→    [REDACTED](16)
   286→    session_dir = _session_dir(session_id)
   287→    session_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   288→
   289→    template_payload = _build_template_payload(packet, session_id=session_id, token=token)
   290→    template_path = session_dir / "review_result.template.json"
   291→    instructions_path = session_dir / "reviewer_instructions.md"
   292→    launch_prompt_path = session_dir / "claude_launch_prompt.md"
   293→    output_path = session_dir / "review_result.json"
   294→    session_path = _session_file(session_id)
   295→
   296→    session_payload = {
   297→        "session_id": session_id,
   298→        "status": "open",
   299→        "runner": runner,
   300→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(now),
   301→        "expires_at": _iso_seconds(expires),
   302→        "ttl_hours": ttl_hours,
   303→        "token": token,
   304→        "attest": EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT,
   305→        "packet_path": str(packet_path),
   306→        "blind_packet_path": str(blind_path),

   308→        "template_path": str(template_path),
   309→        "launch_prompt_path": str(launch_prompt_path),
   310→        "instructions_path": str(instructions_path),
   311→        "expected_output_path": str(output_path),
   312→    }
   313→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   314→    safe_write_text(template_path, json.dumps(template_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   315→    safe_write_text(

   378→
   379→
   380→def _canonical_external_payload(
   381→    raw_payload: dict[str, Any],
   382→    *,
   383→    session: dict[str, Any],
   384→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   385→    """Return import payload with canonical provenance and required session token."""
   386→    session_meta = raw_payload.get("session")
   387→    if not isinstance(session_meta, dict):
   388→        print(
   389→            colorize(
   390→                "  Error: external reviewer payload must include top-level `session` object.",
   391→                "red",
   392→            ),
   393→            file=sys.stderr,
   394→        )
   395→        print(
   396→            colorize(
   397→                "  Expected: {\"session\":{\"id\":\"...\",\"token\":\"...\"},\"assessments\":{...},\"findings\":[...]}",
   398→                "dim",
   399→            ),
   400→            file=sys.stderr,
   401→        )
   402→        sys.exit(1)
   403→
   404→    payload_id = str(session_meta.get("id", "")).strip()
   405→    payload_token = str(session_meta.get("token", "")).strip()
   406→    expected_id = str(session.get("session_id", "")).strip()
   407→    expected_token = str(session.get("token", "")).strip()
   408→    if payload_id != expected_id or payload_token != expected_token:
   409→        print(
   410→            colorize(
   411→                "  Error: session id/token mismatch in external reviewer payload.",
   412→                "red",
   413→            ),
   414→            file=sys.stderr,
   415→        )
   416→        print(
   417→            colorize("  Regenerate output using the session template/instructions.", "yellow"),
   418→            file=sys.stderr,
   419→        )
   420→        sys.exit(1)
   421→
   422→    payload = dict(raw_payload)
   423→    payload.pop("session", None)
   424→    payload.pop("provenance", None)
   425→    payload["provenance"] = {
   426→        "kind": "blind_review_batch_import",
   427→        "blind": True,
   428→        "runner": str(session.get("runner", "claude")),
   429→        "run_stamp": str(session.get("session_id", "")),
   430→        "created_at": _iso_seconds(_utc_now()),
   431→        "packet_path": str(session.get("blind_packet_path", "")),
   432→        "packet_sha256": str(session.get("packet_sha256", "")),
   433→        "external_session_id": expected_id,
   434→    }
   435→    return payload
   436→
   437→
   438→def _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   439→    status = str(session.get("status", "open")).strip().lower()
   440→    if status == "open":
   441→        return
   442→    print(
   443→        colorize(
   444→            f"  Error: session is not open (status={status or 'unknown'}). Start a new session with --external-start.",
   445→            "red",
   446→        ),
   447→        file=sys.stderr,
   448→    )
   449→    sys.exit(1)
   450→
   451→
   452→def _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   453→    expires_at = _parse_iso(session.get("expires_at"))
   454→    if expires_at is None:
   455→        print(
   456→            colorize(
   457→                "  Error: session metadata is missing/invalid expires_at.",
   458→                "red",
   459→            ),
   460→            file=sys.stderr,
   461→        )
   462→        sys.exit(1)
   463→    now = _utc_now()
   464→    if now <= expires_at:
   465→        return
   466→    print(
   467→        colorize(
   468→            f"  Error: session expired at {session.get('expires_at')}. Start a new session with --external-start.",
   469→            "red",
   470→        ),
   471→        file=sys.stderr,
   472→    )
   473→    sys.exit(1)
   474→
   475→
   476→def do_external_submit(
   477→    *,
   478→    import_file: str,
   479→    session_id: str,
   480→    state: dict,
   481→    lang,
   482→    state_file,
   483→    config: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   484→    allow_partial: bool = False,
   485→    scan_after_import: bool = False,
   486→    scan_path: str = ".",
   487→    dry_run: bool = False,
   488→) -> None:
   489→    """Submit external reviewer output via session, adding canonical provenance."""
   490→    config = config or {}
   491→    session_path, session = _session_payload_or_exit(session_id)
   492→    _ensure_session_open_or_exit(session)
   493→    _ensure_session_not_expired_or_exit(session)
   494→
   495→    if str(session.get("runner", "")).strip().lower() not in _EXTERNAL_SUPPORTED_RUNNERS:
   496→        print(
   497→            colorize(
   498→                "  Error: only Claude external sessions currently support durable score submit.",
   499→                "red",
   500→            ),
   501→            file=sys.stderr,
   502→        )
   503→        sys.exit(1)
   504→
   505→    findings_path = Path(import_file)
   506→    raw_payload = _load_json_object(findings_path, label="external findings")
   507→    canonical_payload = _canonical_external_payload(raw_payload, session=session)
   508→
   509→    stamp = runner_helpers_mod.run_stamp()
   510→    session_dir = session_path.parent
   511→    canonical_path = session_dir / f"canonical_import_{stamp}.json"
   512→    safe_write_text(canonical_path, json.dumps(canonical_payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   513→
   514→    if dry_run:
   515→        do_validate_import(
   516→            str(canonical_path),
   517→            lang,
   518→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   519→            attested_external=True,
   520→            manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   521→        )
   522→        return
   523→
   524→    do_import(
   525→        str(canonical_path),
   526→        state,
   527→        lang,
   528→        state_file,
   529→        config=config,
   530→        allow_partial=allow_partial,
   531→        attested_external=True,
   532→        manual_attest=str(session.get("attest", EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT)),
   533→    )
   534→
   535→    submitted_at = _iso_seconds(_utc_now())
   536→    session["status"] = "submitted"
   537→    session["submitted_at"] = submitted_at
   538→    session["submitted_input_file"] = str(findings_path)
   539→    session["submitted_canonical_file"] = str(canonical_path)
   540→    safe_write_text(session_path, json.dumps(session, indent=2) + "\n")
   541→
   542→    if scan_after_import:
   543→        from .batch import FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
   544→
   545→        code = runner_helpers_mod.run_followup_scan(
   546→            lang_name=lang.name,
   547→            scan_path=scan_path,
   548→            deps=runner_helpers_mod.FollowupScanDeps(
   549→                project_root=_runtime_project_root(),
   550→                timeout_seconds=FOLLOWUP_SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   551→                python_executable=sys.executable,
   552→                subprocess_run=subprocess.run,
   553→                timeout_error=subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
   554→                colorize_fn=colorize,
   555→            ),
   556→        )
   557→        if code != 0:
   558→            raise SystemExit(code)
   559→
   560→
   561→__all__ = ["do_external_start", "do_external_submit", "EXTERNAL_ATTEST_TEXT"]
   562→

   100→def _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   101→    *,
   102→    batches: list[dict[str, object]],
   103→    selected_indexes: list[int],
   104→) -> list[str]:
   105→    """Collect normalized file paths reviewed in the selected batch set."""
   106→    reviewed: list[str] = []
   107→    seen: set[str] = set()
   108→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   109→        if idx < 0 or idx >= len(batches):
   110→            continue
   111→        batch = batches[idx]
   112→        files = batch.get("files_to_read", [])
   113→        if not isinstance(files, list):
   114→            continue
   115→        for raw in files:
   116→            if not isinstance(raw, str):
   117→                continue
   118→            path = raw.strip().strip(",'\"")
   119→            if not path or path in {".", ".."}:
   120→                continue
   121→            if path.endswith("/"):
   122→                continue
   123→            if path in seen:
   124→                continue
   125→            seen.add(path)
   126→            reviewed.append(path)
   127→    return reviewed
   128→
   129→
   130→def _coerce_positive_int(value: object, *, default: int, minimum: int = 1) -> int:
   131→    """Parse positive integer CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   132→    if value is None:
   133→        return default
   134→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   135→        return default
   136→    try:
   137→        parsed = int(value)
   138→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   139→        return default
   140→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   141→
   142→
   143→def _coerce_positive_float(value: object, *, default: float, minimum: float = 0.1) -> float:
   144→    """Parse positive float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   145→    if value is None:
   146→        return default
   147→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   148→        return default
   149→    try:
   150→        parsed = float(value)
   151→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   152→        return default
   153→    return parsed if parsed >= minimum else default
   154→
   155→
   156→def _coerce_non_negative_float(value: object, *, default: float) -> float:
   157→    """Parse non-negative float CLI/config inputs with a safe default."""
   158→    if value is None:
   159→        return default
   160→    if not isinstance(value, int | float | str):
   161→        return default
   162→    try:
   163→        parsed = float(value)
   164→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   165→        return default
   166→    return parsed if parsed >= 0.0 else default
   167→
   168→
   169→def _normalize_dimension_list(raw: object) -> list[str]:
   170→    """Normalize dimension collections to a stable, de-duplicated list."""
   171→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
   172→        return []
   173→    out: list[str] = []
   174→    seen: set[str] = set()
   175→    for item in raw:
   176→        if not isinstance(item, str):
   177→            continue
   178→        dim = item.strip()
   179→        if not dim or dim in seen:
   180→            continue
   181→        seen.add(dim)
   182→        out.append(dim)
   183→    return out
   184→

   186→def _scored_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name: str) -> list[str]:
   187→    """Return default scored subjective dimensions for one language."""
   188→    try:
   189→        default_dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   190→    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
   191→        return []
   192→    return _normalize_dimension_list(default_dims)

   194→
   195→def _missing_scored_dimensions(
   196→    *,
   197→    selected_dims: list[str],
   198→    scored_dims: list[str],
   199→) -> list[str]:
   200→    selected = set(selected_dims)
   201→    return [dim for dim in scored_dims if dim not in selected]
   202→
   203→
   204→def _missing_dimensions_command(*, missing_dims: list[str], scan_path: str) -> str:
   205→    """Return rerun command for missing subjective dimensions."""
   206→    base = "desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import"
   207→    if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
   208→        base += f" --path {shlex.quote(scan_path)}"
   209→    if missing_dims:
   210→        base += f" --dimensions {','.join(missing_dims)}"
   211→    return base
   212→
   213→
   214→def _print_preflight_dimension_scope_notice(
   215→    *,
   216→    selected_dims: list[str],
   217→    scored_dims: list[str],
   218→    explicit_selection: bool,
   219→    scan_path: str,
   220→    colorize_fn,
   221→) -> None:
   222→    """Print trigger-time notice when run scope is a scored-dimension subset."""
   223→    if not scored_dims:
   224→        return
   225→    missing_dims = _missing_scored_dimensions(
   226→        selected_dims=selected_dims,

   228→    )
   229→    if not missing_dims:
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    covered_count = len([dim for dim in selected_dims if dim in set(scored_dims)])
   233→    scope_reason = (
   234→        "explicit --dimensions selection"
   235→        if explicit_selection
   236→        else "language default review dimension set"
   237→    )
   238→    tone = "yellow" if explicit_selection else "red"
   239→    print(
   240→        colorize_fn(
   241→            "  WARNING: this run targets "
   242→            f"{covered_count}/{len(scored_dims)} scored subjective dimensions "
   243→            f"({scope_reason}).",
   244→            tone,
   245→        )
   246→    )
   247→    preview = ", ".join(missing_dims[:5])
   248→    if len(missing_dims) > 5:
   249→        preview = f"{preview}, +{len(missing_dims) - 5} more"
   250→    print(colorize_fn(f"  Missing from this run: {preview}", "yellow"))
   251→    print(
   252→        colorize_fn(
   253→            "  Rerun missing dimensions: "
   254→            f"`{_missing_dimensions_command(missing_dims=missing_dims, scan_path=scan_path)}`",
   255→            "dim",
   256→        )
   257→    )
   258→
   259→
   260→def _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   261→    *,
   262→    assessed_dims: list[str],
   263→    scored_dims: list[str],
   264→    scan_path: str,

   300→    args,
   301→    state,

   467→    def _run_batch(*, prompt: str, output_file: Path, log_file: Path) -> int:

   475→    def _make_task(batch_index: int):

   477→        output_path = output_files[batch_index]
   478→        log_path = log_files[batch_index]
   479→
   480→        def _task() -> int:
   481→            try:
   482→                prompt = prompt_path.read_text()
   483→            except OSError as exc:
   484→                raise RuntimeError(
   485→                    f"unable to read prompt for batch #{batch_index + 1}: {prompt_path}"
   486→                ) from exc
   487→            return _run_batch(prompt=prompt, output_file=output_path, log_file=log_path)
   488→
   489→        return _task

   509→            colorize_fn(
   510→                "  Parallel runner config: "
   511→                f"max-workers={min(total_batches, max_parallel_batches)}, "
   512→                f"heartbeat={heartbeat_seconds:.1f}s",
   513→                "dim",
   514→            )
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    def _report_progress(
   518→        batch_index: int,
   519→        event: str,
   520→        code: int | None = None,
   521→        **details,
   522→    ) -> None:
   523→        if event == "heartbeat":
   524→            active = details.get("active_batches")
   525→            queued = details.get("queued_batches", [])
   526→            elapsed = details.get("elapsed_seconds", {})
   527→            if not isinstance(active, list):
   528→                active = []
   529→            if not isinstance(queued, list):
   530→                queued = []
   531→            if not active and not queued:
   532→                return
   533→            segments: list[str] = []
   534→            for idx in active[:6]:
   535→                secs = 0
   536→                if isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   537→                    raw_secs = elapsed.get(idx, 0)
   538→                    secs = int(raw_secs) if isinstance(raw_secs, int | float) else 0
   539→                segments.append(f"#{idx + 1}:{secs}s")
   540→            if len(active) > 6:
   541→                segments.append(f"+{len(active) - 6} more")
   542→            queued_segment = ""
   543→            if queued:
   544→                queued_segment = f", queued {len(queued)}"
   545→            print(
   546→                colorize_fn(
   547→                    "  Batch heartbeat: "
   548→                    f"{len(active)}/{total_batches} active{queued_segment} "
   549→                    f"({', '.join(segments) if segments else 'running batches pending'})",
   550→                    "dim",
   551→                )
   552→            )
   553→            _append_run_log(
   554→                "heartbeat "
   555→                f"active={[idx + 1 for idx in active]} queued={[idx + 1 for idx in queued]} "
   556→                f"elapsed={{{', '.join(f'{idx + 1}:{elapsed.get(idx, 0)}' for idx in active)}}}"
   557→            )
   558→            if stall_warning_seconds > 0 and isinstance(elapsed, dict):
   559→                slow_active = [
   560→                    idx
   561→                    for idx in active
   562→                    if isinstance(elapsed.get(idx), int | float)
   563→                    and int(elapsed.get(idx) or 0) >= stall_warning_seconds
   564→                ]
   565→                newly_warned = [idx for idx in slow_active if idx not in stall_warned_batches]
   566→                if newly_warned:
   567→                    stall_warned_batches.update(newly_warned)
   568→                    warning_message = (
   569→                        "  Stall warning: batches "
   570→                        f"{[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]} exceeded "
   571→                        f"{stall_warning_seconds}s elapsed. "
   572→                        "This may be normal for long runs; review run.log and batch logs."
   573→                    )
   574→                    print(colorize_fn(warning_message, "yellow"))
   575→                    _append_run_log(
   576→                        "stall-warning "
   577→                        f"threshold={stall_warning_seconds}s batches={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(newly_warned)]}"
   578→                    )
   579→            return
   580→
   581→        position = batch_positions.get(batch_index, 0)
   582→        key = str(batch_index + 1)
   583→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   584→            key,
   585→            {
   586→                "position": position,
   587→                "status": "pending",
   588→                "prompt_path": str(prompt_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   589→                "result_path": str(output_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   590→                "log_path": str(log_files.get(batch_index, "")),
   591→            },
   592→        )
   593→        if event == "queued":
   594→            state["status"] = "queued"
   595→            print(
   596→                colorize_fn(
   597→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} queued (#{batch_index + 1})",
   598→                    "dim",
   599→                )
   600→            )
   601→            _append_run_log(f"batch-queued batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   602→            return
   603→        if event == "start":
   604→            state["status"] = "running"
   605→            state["started_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   606→            print(
   607→                colorize_fn(
   608→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} started (#{batch_index + 1})",
   609→                    "dim",
   610→                )
   611→            )
   612→            _append_run_log(f"batch-start batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches}")
   613→            return
   614→        if event == "done":
   615→            status = "done" if code == 0 else f"failed ({code})"
   616→            tone = "dim" if code == 0 else "yellow"
   617→            elapsed_seconds = details.get("elapsed_seconds")
   618→            elapsed_suffix = ""
   619→            if isinstance(elapsed_seconds, int | float):
   620→                elapsed_suffix = f" in {int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))}s"
   621→                state["elapsed_seconds"] = int(max(0, elapsed_seconds))
   622→            state["status"] = "succeeded" if code == 0 else "failed"
   623→            state["exit_code"] = int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else code
   624→            state["completed_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   625→            if batch_index in stall_warned_batches:
   626→                stall_warned_batches.discard(batch_index)
   627→            print(
   628→                colorize_fn(
   629→                    f"  Batch {position}/{total_batches} {status}{elapsed_suffix} (#{batch_index + 1})",
   630→                    tone,
   631→                )
   632→            )
   633→            _append_run_log(
   634→                f"batch-done batch={batch_index + 1} position={position}/{total_batches} "
   635→                f"code={code} elapsed={state.get('elapsed_seconds', 0)}"
   636→            )
   637→
   638→    def _record_execution_issue(batch_index: int, exc: Exception) -> None:
   639→        if batch_index < 0:
   640→            _append_run_log(f"execution-error heartbeat error={exc}")
   641→            return
   642→        _append_run_log(f"execution-error batch={batch_index + 1} error={exc}")
   643→
   644→    run_summary_path = run_dir / "run_summary.json"
   645→
   646→    def _write_run_summary(
   647→        *,
   648→        successful_batches: list[int],
   649→        failed_batches: list[int],
   650→        interrupted: bool = False,
   651→        interruption_reason: str | None = None,
   652→    ) -> None:
   653→        run_summary: dict[str, object] = {
   654→            "created_at": summary_created_at,
   655→            "run_stamp": stamp,
   656→            "runner": runner,
   657→            "parallel": run_parallel,
   658→            "selected_batches": [idx + 1 for idx in selected_indexes],
   659→            "successful_batches": successful_batches,
   660→            "failed_batches": failed_batches,
   661→            "allow_partial": allow_partial,
   662→            "max_parallel_batches": max_parallel_batches if run_parallel else 1,
   663→            "batch_timeout_seconds": batch_timeout_seconds,
   664→            "batch_max_retries": batch_max_retries,
   665→            "batch_retry_backoff_seconds": batch_retry_backoff_seconds,
   666→            "batch_heartbeat_seconds": heartbeat_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   667→            "batch_stall_warning_seconds": stall_warning_seconds if run_parallel else None,
   668→            "batch_stall_kill_seconds": stall_kill_seconds,
   669→            "immutable_packet": str(immutable_packet_path),
   670→            "blind_packet": str(prompt_packet_path),
   671→            "run_dir": str(run_dir),
   672→            "logs_dir": str(logs_dir),
   673→            "run_log": str(run_log_path),
   674→            "batches": batch_status,
   675→        }
   676→        if interrupted:
   677→            run_summary["interrupted"] = True
   678→            if interruption_reason:
   679→                run_summary["interruption_reason"] = interruption_reason
   680→        safe_write_text_fn(run_summary_path, json.dumps(run_summary, indent=2) + "\n")
   681→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Run summary: {run_summary_path}", "dim"))
   682→        _append_run_log(f"run-summary {run_summary_path}")
   683→
   684→    try:
   685→        execution_failures = execute_batches_fn(
   686→            tasks=tasks,
   687→            run_parallel=run_parallel,
   688→            progress_fn=_report_progress,
   689→            error_log_fn=_record_execution_issue,
   690→            max_parallel_workers=max_parallel_batches,
   691→            heartbeat_seconds=heartbeat_seconds,
   692→        )
   693→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   694→        for idx in selected_indexes:
   695→            key = str(idx + 1)
   696→            state = batch_status.setdefault(
   697→                key,
   698→                {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   699→            )
   700→            if state.get("status") in {"pending", "queued", "running"}:
   701→                state["status"] = "interrupted"
   702→        _write_run_summary(
   703→            successful_batches=[],
   704→            failed_batches=[],
   705→            interrupted=True,
   706→            interruption_reason="keyboard_interrupt",
   707→        )
   708→        _append_run_log("run-interrupted reason=keyboard_interrupt")
   709→        raise SystemExit(130) from None
   710→
   711→    allowed_dims = {
   712→        str(dim) for dim in packet.get("dimensions", []) if isinstance(dim, str)
   713→    }
   714→    batch_results, failures = collect_batch_results_fn(
   715→        selected_indexes=selected_indexes,
   716→        failures=execution_failures,
   717→        output_files=output_files,
   718→        allowed_dims=allowed_dims,
   719→    )
   720→
   721→    execution_failure_set = set(execution_failures)
   722→    failure_set = set(failures)
   723→    successful_indexes = sorted(idx for idx in selected_indexes if idx not in failure_set)
   724→    for idx in selected_indexes:
   725→        key = str(idx + 1)
   726→        state = batch_status.setdefault(
   727→            key,
   728→            {"position": batch_positions.get(idx, 0), "status": "pending"},
   729→        )
   730→        if idx not in failure_set:
   731→            state["status"] = "succeeded"
   732→            continue
   733→        if idx in execution_failure_set:
   734→            state["status"] = "failed"
   735→            continue
   736→        if not output_files[idx].exists():
   737→            state["status"] = "missing_output"
   738→            continue
   739→        state["status"] = "parse_failed"
   740→
   741→    _write_run_summary(
   742→        successful_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes],
   743→        failed_batches=[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)],
   744→    )
   745→
   746→    if failures and (not allow_partial or not batch_results):
   747→        _append_run_log(
   748→            f"run-finished failures={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} mode=exit"
   749→        )
   750→        print_failures_and_exit_fn(
   751→            failures=failures,
   752→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   753→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   754→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   755→        )
   756→    elif failures:
   757→        print(
   758→            colorize_fn(
   759→                "  Partial completion enabled: importing successful batches and keeping failed batches open.",
   760→                "yellow",
   761→            )
   762→        )
   763→        print_failures_fn(
   764→            failures=failures,
   765→            packet_path=immutable_packet_path,
   766→            logs_dir=logs_dir,
   767→            colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   768→        )
   769→        _append_run_log(
   770→            "run-partial "
   771→            f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   772→            f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]}"
   773→        )
   774→
   775→    merged = merge_batch_results_fn(batch_results)
   776→    reviewed_files = _collect_reviewed_files_from_batches(
   777→        batches=batches,
   778→        selected_indexes=successful_indexes,
   779→    )
   780→    full_sweep_included = any(
   781→        str(batch.get("name", "")).strip().lower() == "full codebase sweep"
   782→        for idx in successful_indexes
   783→        if 0 <= idx < len(batches)
   784→        for batch in [batches[idx]]
   785→        if isinstance(batch, dict)
   786→    )
   787→    review_scope: dict[str, object] = {
   788→        "reviewed_files_count": len(reviewed_files),
   789→        "successful_batch_count": len(successful_indexes),
   790→        "full_sweep_included": full_sweep_included,
   791→    }
   792→    total_files = packet.get("total_files")
   793→    if isinstance(total_files, int) and not isinstance(total_files, bool) and total_files > 0:
   794→        review_scope["total_files"] = total_files
   795→    merged["review_scope"] = review_scope
   796→    if reviewed_files:
   797→        merged["reviewed_files"] = reviewed_files
   798→        print(
   799→            colorize_fn(
   800→                f"  Reviewed files captured for cache refresh: {len(reviewed_files)}",
   801→                "dim",
   802→            )
   803→        )
   804→    merged["provenance"] = build_import_provenance_fn(
   805→        runner=runner,
   806→        blind_packet_path=prompt_packet_path,
   807→        run_stamp=stamp,
   808→        batch_indexes=successful_indexes,
   809→    )
   810→    merged_assessment_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   811→        list((merged.get("assessments") or {}).keys())
   812→    )
   813→    merged_finding_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   814→        [
   815→            finding.get("dimension")
   816→            for finding in (merged.get("findings") or [])
   817→            if isinstance(finding, dict)
   818→        ]
   819→    )
   820→    merged_imported_dims = _normalize_dimension_list(
   821→        merged_assessment_dims + merged_finding_dims
   822→    )
   823→    review_scope["imported_dimensions"] = merged_imported_dims
   824→    missing_after_import = _print_import_dimension_coverage_notice(
   825→        assessed_dims=merged_assessment_dims,
   826→        scored_dims=scored_dimensions,
   827→        scan_path=scan_path,
   828→        colorize_fn=colorize_fn,
   829→    )
   830→    merged["assessment_coverage"] = {
   831→        "scored_dimensions": scored_dimensions,
   832→        "selected_dimensions": packet_dimensions,
   833→        "imported_dimensions": merged_assessment_dims,
   834→        "missing_dimensions": missing_after_import,
   835→    }
   836→    merged_path = run_dir / "holistic_findings_merged.json"
   837→    safe_write_text_fn(merged_path, json.dumps(merged, indent=2) + "\n")
   838→    print(colorize_fn(f"\n  Merged outputs: {merged_path}", "bold"))
   839→    _print_review_quality(merged.get("review_quality", {}), colorize_fn=colorize_fn)
   840→
   841→    try:
   842→        do_import_fn(
   843→            str(merged_path),
   844→            state,
   845→            lang,
   846→            state_file,
   847→            config=config,
   848→            allow_partial=allow_partial,
   849→            trusted_assessment_source=True,
   850→            trusted_assessment_label="trusted internal run-batches import",
   851→        )
   852→    except SystemExit as exc:
   853→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-failed code={exc.code}")
   854→        raise
   855→    except Exception as exc:
   856→        _append_run_log(f"run-finished import-error error={exc}")
   857→        raise
   858→    _append_run_log(
   859→        "run-finished "
   860→        f"successful={[idx + 1 for idx in successful_indexes]} "
   861→        f"failed={[idx + 1 for idx in sorted(failure_set)]} imported={str(merged_path)}"
   862→    )
   863→
   864→    if getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False):
   865→        followup_code = run_followup_scan_fn(
   866→            lang_name=lang.name,
   867→            scan_path=str(args.path),
   868→        )
   869→        if followup_code != 0:
   870→            print(
   871→                colorize_fn(
   872→                    f"  Follow-up scan failed with exit code {followup_code}.",
   873→                    "red",
   874→                ),
   875→                file=sys.stderr,
   876→            )
   877→            raise SystemExit(followup_code)
   878→
   879→
   880→__all__ = ["do_run_batches"]
   881→

1→"""Tests for the subjective code review system (review.py, commands/review/cmd.py)."""

    21→class TestImportReviewFindings:
    22→    def test_import_valid_findings(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
    23→        diff = import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
    24→        assert diff["new"] == 3
    25→        # Check findings were added to state
    26→        findings = empty_state["findings"]
    27→        assert len(findings) == 3
    28→        # Check finding IDs follow the pattern
    29→        ids = list(findings.keys())
    30→        assert any("naming_quality" in fid for fid in ids)
    31→        assert any("comment_quality" in fid for fid in ids)
    32→        assert any("error_consistency" in fid for fid in ids)
    33→
    34→    def test_import_skips_malformed_findings(self, empty_state):
    35→        data = [
    36→            {"file": "foo.ts"},  # Missing required fields
    37→            {"dimension": "naming_quality"},  # Missing file
    38→            {  # Valid
    39→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
    40→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
    41→                "identifier": "foo",
    42→                "summary": "test",
    43→                "confidence": "low",
    44→            },
    45→        ]
    46→        diff = import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(data), empty_state, "typescript")
    47→        assert diff["new"] == 1
    48→
    49→    def test_import_validates_confidence(self, empty_state):
    50→        data = [
    51→            {
    52→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
    53→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
    54→                "identifier": "foo",
    55→                "summary": "test",
    56→                "confidence": "very_high",  # Invalid
    57→            }
    58→        ]
    59→        import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(data), empty_state, "typescript")
    60→        finding = list(empty_state["findings"].values())[0]
    61→        assert finding["confidence"] == "low"
    62→
    63→    def test_import_validates_dimension(self, empty_state):
    64→        data = [
    65→            {

    76→    def test_import_updates_review_cache(
    77→        self, empty_state, sample_findings_data, tmp_path
    78→    ):
    79→        # Create actual files so hashing works
    80→        (tmp_path / "src").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    81→        with patch("desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.per_file.PROJECT_ROOT", tmp_path):
    82→            (tmp_path / "src" / "foo.ts").write_text("content")
    83→            (tmp_path / "src" / "bar.ts").write_text("content")
    84→            import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
    85→        cache = empty_state.get("review_cache", {}).get("files", {})
    86→        assert len(cache) >= 1  # At least one file cached
    87→
    88→    def test_import_merges_with_state(self, state_with_findings, sample_findings_data):

    96→        self, empty_state, sample_findings_data

   106→    def test_import_preserves_wontfix_findings(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   107→        # First import
   108→        import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
   109→        # Mark one as wontfix
   110→        for f in empty_state["findings"].values():
   111→            if "naming_quality" in f["id"]:
   112→                f["status"] = "wontfix"
   113→                f["note"] = "intentionally generic"
   114→                break
   115→        # Second import with same findings
   116→        import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
   117→        # Wontfix should NOT be auto-resolved (it's still in current findings)
   118→        assert any(f["status"] == "wontfix" for f in empty_state["findings"].values())
   119→        # The finding still exists
   120→        assert any(
   121→            "naming_quality" in f["id"] for f in empty_state["findings"].values()
   122→        )
   123→

   140→    def test_id_collision_different_summaries(self, empty_state):
   141→        """Two findings for same file/dimension/identifier but different summaries
   142→        must both appear in state (#56)."""
   143→        data = [
   144→            {
   145→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
   146→                "dimension": "naming_quality",

   149→                "evidence_lines": [15],
   150→                "confidence": "high",
   151→            },
   152→            {
   153→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
   154→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
   155→                "identifier": "processData",

   157→                "evidence_lines": [15],

   159→            },

   165→    def test_id_stable_for_same_summary(self, empty_state):
   166→        """Same summary should produce the same finding ID (stable hash)."""
   167→        data = [
   168→            {
   169→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
   170→                "dimension": "naming_quality",

   187→
   188→class TestScoringIntegration:
   189→    def test_review_findings_appear_in_scoring(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   190→        import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
   191→
   192→        # Assessment scores drive dimension scores directly.
   193→        # Review findings are tracked but don't affect the score.
   194→        assessments = {
   195→            "naming_quality": {"score": 75},
   196→            "comment_quality": {"score": 85},
   197→        }
   198→        potentials = {"review": 2}
   199→        dim_scores = compute_dimension_scores(
   200→            empty_state["findings"], potentials, subjective_assessments=assessments
   201→        )
   202→        assert "Naming quality" in dim_scores
   203→        assert dim_scores["Naming quality"]["score"] == 75.0
   204→        det = dim_scores["Naming quality"]["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]
   205→        assert det["assessment_score"] == 75.0
   206→
   207→    def test_review_findings_not_auto_resolved_by_scan(
   208→        self, empty_state, sample_findings_data
   209→    ):
   210→        # Import review findings
   211→        import_review_findings(_as_review_payload(sample_findings_data), empty_state, "typescript")
   212→        review_ids = {
   213→            f["id"]
   214→            for f in empty_state["findings"].values()
   215→            if f["detector"] == "review"
   216→        }
   217→
   218→        # Simulate a normal scan with no review detector in potentials
   219→        merge_scan(
   220→            empty_state,
   221→            [],
   222→            options=MergeScanOptions(
   223→                lang="typescript",
   224→                potentials={"unused": 10, "smells": 50},
   225→            ),
   226→        )
   227→
   228→        # Review findings should still be open (not auto-resolved)
   229→        for fid in review_ids:
   230→            if fid in empty_state["findings"]:
   231→                assert empty_state["findings"][fid]["status"] == "open"
   232→
   233→    def test_review_in_file_based_detectors(self):
   234→        assert "review" in FILE_BASED_DETECTORS
   235→
   236→    def test_test_health_dimension_exists(self):
   237→        dim_names = [d.name for d in DIMENSIONS]
   238→        assert "Test health" in dim_names
   239→        rc = [d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == "Test health"][0]
   240→        assert rc.tier == 4
