```
1→"""CLI entrypoint for review command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .batch import _do_run_batches
    13→from .external import do_external_start, do_external_submit
    14→from .import_cmd import do_import, do_validate_import
    15→from .prepare import do_prepare
    16→
    17→
    18→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    19→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    20→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    21→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    22→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    23→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    24→            continue
    25→        try:
    26→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    27→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→            continue
    29→
    30→
    31→def cmd_review(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Prepare or import subjective code review findings."""
    33→    _enable_live_review_output()
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state_file = runtime.state_path
    36→    state = runtime.state
    37→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    38→
    39→    if not lang:
    40→        print(
    41→            colorize("  Error: could not detect language. Use --lang.", "red"),
    42→            file=sys.stderr,
    43→        )
    44→        sys.exit(1)
    45→
    46→    merge = bool(getattr(args, "merge", False))
    47→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    48→    external_start = bool(getattr(args, "external_start", False))
    49→    external_submit = bool(getattr(args, "external_submit", False))
    50→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    51→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    52→
    53→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    54→    mode_flags = [
    55→        merge,
    56→        run_batches,
    57→        external_start,
    58→        external_submit,
    59→        import_mode,
    60→        bool(validate_import_file),
    61→    ]
    62→    if sum(1 for enabled in mode_flags if enabled) > 1:
    63→        print(
    64→            colorize(
    65→                "  Error: choose one review mode per command "
    66→                "(--merge | --run-batches | --external-start | --external-submit | --import | --validate-import).",
    67→                "red",
    68→            ),
    69→            file=sys.stderr,
    70→        )
    71→        sys.exit(1)
    72→
    73→    if external_submit and not import_file:
    74→        print(
    75→            colorize(
    76→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --import FILE.",
    77→                "red",
    78→            ),
    79→            file=sys.stderr,
    80→        )
    81→        sys.exit(2)
    82→
    83→    if external_submit and not getattr(args, "session_id", None):
    84→        print(
    85→            colorize(
    86→                "  Error: --external-submit requires --session-id.",
    87→                "red",
    88→            ),
    89→            file=sys.stderr,
    90→        )
    91→        sys.exit(2)
    92→
    93→    if merge:
    94→        from desloppify.app.commands.review.merge import do_merge
    95→
    96→        do_merge(args)
    97→        return
    98→
    99→    if run_batches:
   100→        _do_run_batches(
   101→            args,
   102→            state,
   103→            lang,
   104→            state_file,
   105→            config=runtime.config,
   106→        )
   107→        return
   108→
   109→    if external_start:
   110→        do_external_start(
   111→            args,
   112→            state,
   113→            lang,
   114→            config=runtime.config,
   115→        )
   116→        return
   117→
   118→    if external_submit:
   119→        do_external_submit(
   120→            import_file=str(import_file),
   121→            session_id=str(getattr(args, "session_id")),
   122→            state=state,
   123→            lang=lang,
   124→            state_file=state_file,
   125→            config=runtime.config,
   126→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   127→            scan_after_import=bool(getattr(args, "scan_after_import", False)),
   128→            scan_path=str(getattr(args, "path", ".") or "."),
   129→            dry_run=bool(getattr(args, "dry_run", False)),
   130→        )
   131→        return
   132→
   133→    if validate_import_file:
   134→        do_validate_import(
   135→            validate_import_file,
   136→            lang,
   137→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   138→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   139→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   140→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   141→        )
   142→        return
   143→
   144→    if import_file:
   145→        do_import(
   146→            import_file,
   147→            state,
   148→            lang,
   149→            state_file,
   150→            config=runtime.config,
   151→            allow_partial=bool(getattr(args, "allow_partial", False)),
   152→            manual_override=bool(getattr(args, "manual_override", False)),
   153→            attested_external=bool(getattr(args, "attested_external", False)),
   154→            manual_attest=getattr(args, "attest", None),
   155→        )
   156→    else:
   157→        do_prepare(args, state, lang, state_file, config=runtime.config)
   158→
```

```
title:	Auto-refresh and report updated score/scorecard after all score-changing operations
state:	OPEN
author:	peteromallet
labels:	
comments:	0
assignees:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	160
--
## Summary
We should automatically recompute and report the latest score (and scorecard) whenever a workflow step materially changes scoring state.

Right now this is too easy to leave stale. You do the work (import reviews, resolve findings, mark wontfix, etc.) but don’t immediately see the new truth unless you manually re-scan or run another command.

## Why this matters
- Score trust: stale score output makes it hard to trust progress.
- Workflow momentum: users should see immediate feedback after score-moving actions.
- Better prioritization: the very next decision (plan/next/fix) should use current score, not old score.

## Proposed behavior
After any operation that can change objective/strict/subjective scoring, automatically:
1. Refresh scoring state (incremental where possible, full recompute fallback).
2. Print a concise delta summary (`before -> after`) for key scores.
3. Render/update scorecard output in the same command response.
4. Persist updated score artifacts (`query.json`, scorecard snapshot, state fields) so follow-up commands see current values.

## Trigger points (minimum)
- `desloppify review --import ...`
- `desloppify review --run-batches ...` (post-merge/import path)
- `desloppify review --external-submit ...` (trusted external import path)
- `desloppify resolve fixed ...`
- `desloppify resolve wontfix ...`
- `desloppify resolve false_positive ...` / reopen transitions
- Any batch/command that applies auto-fixes and resolves findings as fixed

## Output expectations
For score-moving commands, include a short “score update” block at end:
- Overall (lenient + strict)
- Objective + Subjective totals
- Dimension deltas where changed (compact)
- Wontfix debt signal (strict gap)

Example shape:
- `Strict: 82.4 -> 84.1 (+1.7)`
- `Lenient: 90.2 -> 90.4 (+0.2)`
- `Subjective: 71.0 -> 74.8 (+3.8)`
- `Changed dimensions: naming_quality +6.0, error_consistency -1.0`

## Implementation approaches
### A) Centralized post-mutation hook (recommended)
Add one shared `post_score_mutation_update(...)` helper invoked by all score-changing commands.
- Pros: consistent behavior, easier to maintain, no drift between commands.
- Cons: requires command touchpoints audit.

### B) Event-driven state mutation bus
Emit “score-affecting event” from each command and have a single score updater subscriber.
- Pros: scalable, cleaner architecture for future telemetry.
- Cons: larger refactor.

### C) Command-local recalculation only
Each command recalculates and prints scores itself.
- Pros: quick patch.
- Cons: drift risk, duplicate logic, inconsistent UX.

## Suggested rollout
1. Implement centralized helper + wire into review import and resolve flows first.
2. Add score-delta block + scorecard refresh output.
3. Expand to all fix/apply flows that mutate finding status.
4. Add guard tests for every trigger point.

## Acceptance criteria
- Every listed trigger point emits updated score output in the same command run.
- `query.json` and persisted score state are updated immediately after each trigger.
- No stale score reported after successful imports/resolutions.
- Regression tests cover all trigger paths and ensure score delta block appears.
- Performance remains acceptable (incremental update path preferred, full recompute fallback).

## Nice-to-have
- Add `--no-score-refresh` escape hatch for power users/scripts.
- Add structured JSON score delta payload for machine consumers.
```

## Detailed Implementation Plan: Auto-refresh Score Reporting (Issue #160)

### Architecture Summary

The core insight from exploring the codebase: **all score-changing commands already recompute scores via `save_state()` which calls `_recompute_stats()`**. The gap is strictly in **reporting** and **query.json persistence**. The implementation creates a single centralized helper and wires it into the two commands that lack score delta reporting: `review --import` and `review --merge`.

### Phase 1: Create the centralized helper

**New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/score_update.py`**

This module will contain one primary function plus a supporting dict-builder for query payloads.

```python
"""Centralized score-delta display for all score-changing operations."""

from __future__ import annotations

from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.state import ScoreSnapshot
from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import _format_delta
from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    format_strict_target_progress,
)
from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize


def print_score_update(
    prev: ScoreSnapshot,
    state: dict,
    *,
    strict_target: dict | None = None,
    command: str | None = None,
) -> ScoreSnapshot:
    """Print compact score delta block after a score-changing operation.

    Parameters
    ----------
    prev : ScoreSnapshot
        Snapshot taken before the mutation.
    state : dict
        The current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    strict_target : dict or None
        The ``strict_target`` dict from ``compute_narrative()["strict_target"]``.
        When provided, strict target progress is displayed.
    command : str or None
        Name of the calling command (for logging/diagnostics, not displayed).

    Returns
    -------
    ScoreSnapshot
        The new snapshot (for use in query payloads).
    """
    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    if (
        new.overall is None
        or new.objective is None
        or new.strict is None
        or new.verified is None
    ):
        print(
            colorize(
                "  Scores unavailable -- run a full scan first.",
                "yellow",
            )
        )
        return new

    # Strict score with delta (primary north-star metric)
    strict_delta_str, strict_color = _format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    overall_delta_str, overall_color = _format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    objective_delta_str, _ = _format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    verified_delta_str, _ = _format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)

    print(
        "  Scores: "
        + colorize(
            f"strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}",
            strict_color,
        )
        + colorize(
            f"  overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}",
            overall_color,
        )
        + colorize(
            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
            "dim",
        )
        + colorize(
            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
            "dim",
        )
    )

    # Strict target progress (the constant north-star reminder)
    if isinstance(strict_target, dict):
        lines, _target, _gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
        for message, style in lines:
            print(colorize(message, style))

    # Wontfix debt gap
    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
        print(
            colorize(
                f"  {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict -- "
                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
                "yellow",
            )
        )

    return new


def score_update_query_fields(
    prev: ScoreSnapshot,
    new: ScoreSnapshot,
) -> dict:
    """Return dict of score fields suitable for merging into a query payload."""
    return {
        "overall_score": new.overall,
        "objective_score": new.objective,
        "strict_score": new.strict,
        "verified_strict_score": new.verified,
        "prev_overall_score": prev.overall,
        "prev_objective_score": prev.objective,
        "prev_strict_score": prev.strict,
        "prev_verified_strict_score": prev.verified,
    }


__all__ = [
    "print_score_update",
    "score_update_query_fields",
]
```

Key design decisions:
- **Strict-first ordering**: The issue states "the strict score and the target score should be constantly communicated." Strict comes first in the output line.
- **Reuses `_format_delta`** from `scan_helpers.py` for consistent delta formatting with color.
- **Reuses `format_strict_target_progress`** from `strict_target.py` for target progress rendering.
- **Returns `ScoreSnapshot`** so callers can use it for query payloads without calling `score_snapshot` again.
- **`score_update_query_fields`** is a separate function to build the standard score fields for query.json payloads.
- **`strict_target`** is passed in rather than computed internally, because `compute_narrative()` is already called by all callers and returns this value.

Wait -- there is a subtlety. The `do_merge` function computes narrative at line 116, **before** the merge happens. But `compute_narrative` uses the pre-mutation state for strict_target. The strict_target itself only depends on the config's `target_strict_score` and the current strict score, so if strict score changes from the merge, the target dict will have the old `current` value. For strict_target to be accurate, we should recompute it after the merge.

The simplest fix: recompute narrative after save, or compute strict_target directly:

### Phase 4: Update `import_helpers.py` -- mark old function for reference

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/import_helpers.py`**

The function `print_review_import_scores_and_integrity` (lines 923-974) is currently only called from `do_import` in `import_cmd.py`. After Phase 2, it will no longer be called. We should:

1. **Keep the function** (don't delete yet) but mark it as deprecated, OR
2. **Extract just the integrity warning** into a separate function.

The cleanest approach: extract the subjective integrity check into its own function, since it's a distinct concern from score display:

```python
def print_subjective_at_target_warning(
    at_target: list[dict],
    *,
    subjective_rerun_command_fn,
    colorize_fn,
) -> None:
    """Warn when subjective dimension scores match the target score."""
    if not at_target:
        return
    count = len(at_target)
    command = subjective_rerun_command_fn(at_target, max_items=5)
    if count >= 2:
        print(
            colorize_fn(
                "  WARNING: "
                f"{count} subjective scores match the target score. "
                "On the next scan, those dimensions will be reset to 0.0 by the anti-gaming safeguard "
                f"unless you rerun and re-import objective reviews first: {command}",
                "red",
            )
        )
    else:
        print(
            colorize_fn(
                "  WARNING: "
                f"{count} subjective score matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
                f"Can you rerun it by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
                "yellow",
            )
        )
```

Add `"print_subjective_at_target_warning"` to the `__all__` list in `import_helpers.py`.

Then in `import_cmd.py`, the replacement for the old call becomes:

```python
    # Score delta (centralized)
    new = print_score_update(
        prev,
        state,
        strict_target=narrative.get("strict_target"),
    )

    # Subjective integrity warning
    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config or {}, fallback=95.0)
    at_target = _SCORECARD_SUBJECTIVE_AT_TARGET(
        state,
        state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
        target=target_strict,
    )
    import_helpers_mod.print_subjective_at_target_warning(
        at_target,
        subjective_rerun_command_fn=reporting_dimensions_mod.subjective_rerun_command,
        colorize_fn=colorize,
    )
```

### Phase 6: Tests

**New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_score_update.py`**

```python
"""Tests for centralized score update display helper."""

from __future__ import annotations

from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import (
    print_score_update,
    score_update_query_fields,
)
from desloppify.state import ScoreSnapshot, empty_state, save_state


class TestPrintScoreUpdate:
    def test_prints_all_four_scores(self, capsys, tmp_path):
        """Score update block includes strict, overall, objective, verified."""
        state = empty_state()
        state["stats"] = {
            "total": 100, "open": 10, "fixed": 80, "wontfix": 5,
            "false_positive": 5, "auto_resolved": 0,
        }
        # Simulate scores being set
        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=80.0, objective=75.0, strict=70.0, verified=65.0)
        # Force known scores into state
        state["stats"]["overall_score"] = 82.0
        state["stats"]["objective_score"] = 77.0
        state["stats"]["strict_score"] = 72.0
        state["stats"]["verified_strict_score"] = 67.0

        new = print_score_update(prev, state)
        out = capsys.readouterr().out
        assert "strict" in out
        assert "overall" in out
        assert "objective" in out
        assert "verified" in out

    def test_prints_deltas_when_scores_change(self, capsys):
        """Positive and negative deltas are shown."""
        state = empty_state()
        state["stats"] = {
            "total": 100, "open": 10, "fixed": 80, "wontfix": 5,
            "false_positive": 5, "auto_resolved": 0,
            "overall_score": 85.0,
            "objective_score": 80.0,
            "strict_score": 75.0,
            "verified_strict_score": 70.0,
        }
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=83.0, objective=80.0, strict=73.0, verified=70.0)
        print_score_update(prev, state)
        out = capsys.readouterr().out
        assert "(+2.0)" in out or "+2.0" in out  # strict delta

    def test_shows_strict_target_progress(self, capsys):
        """Strict target progress is shown when strict_target dict provided."""
        state = empty_state()
        state["stats"] = {
            "total": 100, "open": 10, "fixed": 80, "wontfix": 5,
            "false_positive": 5, "auto_resolved": 0,
            "overall_score": 85.0,
            "objective_score": 80.0,
            "strict_score": 75.0,
            "verified_strict_score": 70.0,
        }
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=85.0, objective=80.0, strict=75.0, verified=70.0)
        strict_target = {
            "target": 95.0,
            "current": 75.0,
            "gap": 20.0,
            "state": "below",
        }
        print_score_update(prev, state, strict_target=strict_target)
        out = capsys.readouterr().out
        assert "95.0" in out  # target
        assert "below" in out.lower() or "20.0" in out  # gap info

    def test_returns_new_snapshot(self):
        """Function returns the new ScoreSnapshot."""
        state = empty_state()
        state["stats"] = {
            "total": 10, "open": 1, "fixed": 9, "wontfix": 0,
            "false_positive": 0, "auto_resolved": 0,
            "overall_score": 90.0,
            "objective_score": 88.0,
            "strict_score": 85.0,
            "verified_strict_score": 82.0,
        }
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=88.0, objective=86.0, strict=83.0, verified=80.0)
        new = print_score_update(prev, state)
        assert isinstance(new, ScoreSnapshot)

    def test_unavailable_scores_prints_warning(self, capsys):
        """When scores are None, prints warning instead."""
        state = empty_state()
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=None, objective=None, strict=None, verified=None)
        print_score_update(prev, state)
        out = capsys.readouterr().out
        assert "unavailable" in out.lower()

    def test_wontfix_debt_warning(self, capsys):
        """Large wontfix gap shows debt warning."""
        state = empty_state()
        state["stats"] = {
            "total": 100, "open": 10, "fixed": 60, "wontfix": 20,
            "false_positive": 10, "auto_resolved": 0,
            "overall_score": 90.0,
            "objective_score": 85.0,
            "strict_score": 80.0,
            "verified_strict_score": 75.0,
        }
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=90.0, objective=85.0, strict=80.0, verified=75.0)
        print_score_update(prev, state)
        out = capsys.readouterr().out
        assert "wontfix" in out.lower() or "debt" in out.lower()


class TestScoreUpdateQueryFields:
    def test_returns_all_eight_fields(self):
        """Query fields dict has all 8 score keys."""
        prev = ScoreSnapshot(overall=80.0, objective=75.0, strict=70.0, verified=65.0)
        new = ScoreSnapshot(overall=82.0, objective=77.0, strict=72.0, verified=67.0)
        fields = score_update_query_fields(prev, new)
        assert "overall_score" in fields
        assert "strict_score" in fields
        assert "objective_score" in fields
        assert "verified_strict_score" in fields
        assert "prev_overall_score" in fields
        assert "prev_strict_score" in fields
        assert "prev_objective_score" in fields
        assert "prev_verified_strict_score" in fields
        assert fields["strict_score"] == 72.0
        assert fields["prev_strict_score"] == 70.0
```

| `desloppify/app/commands/fix/apply_flow.py` | MODIFY | Replace ad-hoc score printing with centralized helper |

| `desloppify/tests/commands/test_score_update.py` | **NEW** | Unit tests for the centralized helper |

### Implementation Sequence

1. Create `score_update.py` with `print_score_update()` and `score_update_query_fields()`
2. Extract `print_subjective_at_target_warning()` in `import_helpers.py`
3. Wire into `import_cmd.py` (highest priority -- most common user flow)
4. Wire into `merge.py` (second priority -- currently shows NO scores)
5. Wire into `fix/apply_flow.py` (standardize existing but inconsistent reporting)
6. Wire into `resolve/cmd.py` (`cmd_ignore_pattern` -- no-delta display)
7. Add tests
8. Run full test suite: `python -m pytest desloppify/tests/ -q`

### Potential Challenges

1. **Score computation timing in `import_cmd.py`**: The `save_state()` call at line 265 triggers `_recompute_stats()`. The `score_snapshot()` call after that will see updated scores. The `prev` snapshot must be taken before any mutation (before `working_state` operations). Since `do_import` copies state into `working_state` and then does `state.clear(); state.update(working_state)` at lines 263-264, the `prev` snapshot should be captured from the original `state` before that clear/update happens. Capturing it at the start of the function (right after the validation block) is safe because `state` hasn't been mutated yet.

2. **`do_merge`'s narrative timing**: Currently `do_merge` computes narrative before the merge for use in the query payload. Moving it after `save_state` is correct because:
   - The narrative should reflect post-merge state
   - It's only used in the `write_query` call at the end
   - The early-return paths (lines 119, 135, 167) don't use narrative for `write_query`

3. **`_format_delta` is a "private" function** (prefixed with `_`) in `scan_helpers.py` but is already exported in `__all__`. Importing it from `score_update.py` follows the existing pattern (it's already imported by `scan_reporting_summary.py`).

4. **Backwards compatibility of `print_review_import_scores_and_integrity`**: Keep the old function but stop calling it from `do_import`. If any external code references it, it remains importable. Alternatively, have it delegate to the new helper for the score portion and keep the integrity check inline.

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/import_cmd.py` - Wire in before/after snapshot and replace score display in do_import()
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/merge.py` - Wire in score reporting (currently has NO score output at all)

   - Fix 1: Stale marking precision (#164/#161) - `upsert_findings()` and `auto_resolve_disappeared()` were tracking ALL detectors in a scan rather than only those with actual changes, causing false stale marking
   - Fix 2: Subjective review reopen guard (#158/#156) - `subjective_review` findings could reopen after being auto-resolved by holistic import, creating a loop
   - Fix 3: Gate stale-review nudges behind cleared queue - Stop pushing users to re-review stale subjective dimensions while mechanical findings are still open
   - Fix 4: Hash-only finding ID lookup (#163) - `desloppify show <8-char-hash>` couldn't find findings
   - Fix 5: Windows zone_overrides path normalization (#159) - absolute paths in zone_set didn't match rel() form at scan time

From the user: "figure out what are all the points of which we could theoretically be recalculating the score and updating the scorecard and even communicating the change to people and then figure out how we can refactor this just such that this is still like a nice and elegant part of the code base."

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Score quartet:** overall_score, objective_score, strict_score, verified_strict_score (all 0-100)
   - **`_recompute_stats()`** in `scoring.py` - main score recomputation orchestrator
   - **`save_state()`** always calls `_recompute_stats()` - so scores are always fresh after save
   - **`ScoreSnapshot`** NamedTuple with (overall, objective, strict, verified) - used for before/after comparison
   - **`score_snapshot(state)`** - loads all 4 scores in one call
   - **`_format_delta(value, prev)`** in `scan_helpers.py` - returns `(delta_str, color)` for colored score change display
   - **`format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)`** in `strict_target.py` - renders target progress
   - **`strict_target` dict** - computed by `compute_narrative()`, contains target/current/gap/state
   - **`target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)`** - reads target from config
   - **Dimension-based scoring** with mechanical (40%) and subjective (60%) pools
   - **Finding lifecycle:** open → fixed/auto_resolved/wontfix/false_positive, with reopen capability
   - **`changed_detectors` tracking** - new pattern introduced in Fix 1 to precisely track which detectors had actual changes
   - **`agent_import` attestation** - resolution attestation kind used by holistic review imports
   - **`CommandRuntime`** dataclass with config, state, state_path
   - **Atomic state persistence** - temp file + `os.replace()` with `.json.bak` backups

3. Files and Code Sections:

   **Files Modified in Fix 1-5:**

   - **`desloppify/engine/_state/merge_findings.py`** - Core finding upsert/auto-resolve logic
     - Fix 1: Added `changed_detectors: set[str]` tracking to both `upsert_findings()` and `auto_resolve_disappeared()`
     - Fix 2: Added reopen guard for `subjective_review` + `auto_resolved` + `agent_import` attestation
     - Changed return types: `upsert_findings()` now returns 6-tuple (added `changed_detectors`), `auto_resolve_disappeared()` now returns 4-tuple (added `resolved_detectors`)
     ```python
     def auto_resolve_disappeared(...) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
         """..."""
         resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
         resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
         # ... in resolution block:
         resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
         resolved += 1
         return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors

     def upsert_findings(...) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
         """..."""
         changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
         # ... on new finding:
         new_count += 1
         changed_detectors.add(detector)
         # ... on reopen (with Fix 2 guard):
         if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
             if (
                 detector == "subjective_review"
                 and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
                 and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
             ):
                 continue
             # ... existing reopen logic ...
             reopened_count += 1
             changed_detectors.add(detector)
         return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
     ```

   - **`desloppify/engine/_state/merge.py`** - Scan merge operations
     - Fix 1: Updated unpacking to get `upsert_changed` and `resolve_changed`, replaced `changed_detectors = set(current_by_detector.keys())` with `changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed`
     ```python
     current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
         upsert_findings(...)
     )
     auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(...)
     changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
     if changed_detectors:
         _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now)
     ```

     ```python
             return []

     ```

     ```python
     # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
     if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
         return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
     ```

     - Fix 5: Normalized paths through `rel()` in `_zone_set()` and `_zone_clear()`
     ```python
     def _zone_set(args):
         filepath = args.zone_path
         normalized = rel(filepath)
         config = command_runtime(args).config
         config.setdefault("zone_overrides", {})[normalized] = zone_value
         # ...
         print(f"  Set {normalized} → {zone_value}")

     def _zone_clear(args):
         filepath = args.zone_path
         normalized = rel(filepath)
         # ...
         if normalized in overrides:
             del overrides[normalized]
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Test directory location:** Initially tried `tests/` at project root, but tests are at `desloppify/tests/`. Found via glob search.
   - **No errors in implementation:** All 5 fixes and 19 tests passed on first run (3145 passed, 3 skipped).
   - **Zone test `rel` monkeypatch:** After adding `rel()` normalization in Fix 5, existing zone tests needed `rel` monkeypatched to identity function to avoid path resolution issues in test environment. Added `monkeypatch.setattr("desloppify.app.commands.zone_cmd.rel", lambda p: p)` to affected tests.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Fix 1 precision problem:** The root cause was `changed_detectors = set(current_by_detector.keys())` using ALL detectors present, not just changed ones. Solved by tracking actual changes in both `upsert_findings()` (new/reopened) and `auto_resolve_disappeared()` (resolved), then using the union.
   - **Fix 2 reopen loop:** Condition-based `subjective_review` findings would be regenerated by detection on the next scan, hitting the reopen path. Solved by checking for `detector == "subjective_review"` + `status == "auto_resolved"` + `attestation.kind == "agent_import"`.
   - **Fix 3 churn loop:** Users were being pushed to re-review while mechanical findings were still open. Solved by adding `_has_open_findings()` gate to `_stale_assessment_reminder()` and the stale hint blocks in scan reporting and status rendering.
   - **Issue #160 analysis:** Discovered that all commands already recompute scores (via `save_state()` → `_recompute_stats()`). The gap is strictly in reporting deltas and persisting scores in query.json. The review commands (`--import`, `--merge`, `--external-submit`, `--run-batches`) are the primary gaps.
